### Page 3

2
結論
結論
記憶體產業歷經庫存調整與價格下修後，走出谷底進入上升週期。
AI 應用快速擴展下HBM、DDR5產能持續緊俏，供不應求走勢延續。
全球伺服器市場及AI推論需求擴大，帶動儲存市場規模擴大
推薦個股：南亞科(2408)、華邦電(2344)、群聯(8299)、宜鼎(5289)。

### Page 4

3
受AI爆炸性成長打破循環
DRAM市場過往約每3～4年形成一個完整供需週期。隨AI需求爆炸性成長，三大原廠相繼EOL舊
世代產品轉向全力生產高階產品導致排擠 ，因此導致價格直線式飆升，當價格上漲至一定程度時，
消費性產品轉嫁能力有限導致需求減少 ，然而AI需求仍維持強勁導致供給無法滿足需求 ，預期至
2027年仍維持供不應求態勢。
資料來源：Trendforce，兆豐投顧整理
DRAM 供需比
AI需求延續打破循環

### Page 5

4
預期DRAM價格維持高檔
DRAM 市場在 2025 年下半年迎來顯著的價格攀升，2026年全年DDR4、DDR5價格持續走高，
AI持續爆炸性成長之下，持續搶佔記憶體顆粒產品，雖消費性產品已開始面臨價格轉嫁困境導致
拉貨力道減弱，但記憶體顆粒廠商仍無法滿足 AI需求，因此預期記憶體價格將維持高檔 ，然LTA
比例提高預期漲價幅度將逐步放緩。
資料來源：Trendforce，兆豐投顧彙整
DRAM價格走勢
AI需求延續打破循環

全球DRAM市場佔有率中，Samsung 以 39.4% 穩居龍頭寶座，SK hynix 以 24.9% 位居第二，

儲（CXMT）市佔率持續快速攀升至 9.5%，進一步壓縮其他廠商空間，而南亞科（Nanya）、
華邦電（Winbond）與力積電（PSMC）則分別以 1.7%、0.6% 與 0.1% 的市佔率分列第五至
第七名。

受記憶體價格上漲衝擊，PC 與手機等消費性終端產品需求受到壓抑，2026 年至 2027 年出貨
量預估持續呈現負成長，然而伺服器強勁出貨動能帶動下，2026、2027年出貨量年增率分別達

達 13% 與 17%，伺服器佔整體記憶體用量比重持續攀升逾40%，龐大的運算需求有效填補並推
升用量缺口，促使整體DRAM市場維持供不應求的緊俏態勢。
AI需求延續打破循環

### Page 9

8
記憶體製程持續推進
資料來源： 兆豐投顧整理
記憶體廠商持續推進微縮製程，核心目的在於縮小晶片面積以增加每片晶圓的晶片產出量、顯
著降低每單位容量的生產成本，並同時實現更高儲存容量、更快傳輸速度與更低功耗，以搶佔
AI 與新世代伺服器等高階市場的競爭優勢。
DRAM Process Node Roadmap
AI需求延續打破循環

### Page 10

9
打破記憶體牆（Memory Wall）
資料來源：KAIST TERALAB、兆豐投顧整理
AI 大型語言模型（LLM）參數持續擴大下，GPU 運算核心的算力成長遠快於記憶體存取速度，
單純靠提高傳輸頻率來擴展頻寬已面臨物理瓶頸，因此，HBM4 選擇將 I/O 匯流排寬度從行之
已久的 1,024-bit 翻倍至 2,048-bit，將顯著提升算力利用率。並且HBM3E消耗3倍的DDR5晶圓。
HBM4消耗3.5倍，HBM4E會消耗5倍的DDR5晶圓，HBM持續迭代下將會消耗更多記憶體晶圓。
HBM製程進度
AI需求延續打破循環

### Page 11

10
DRAM原廠擴產有限
資料來源： Trendforce、兆豐投顧整理
原廠於2027年擴產力道有限，而AI Server 搭載的 HBM結構複雜，生產過程將大量消耗先進製
程產能，同時NVIDIA新型伺服器搭載大量LPDDR5，預計將占盡高階產能將近 70%。在總體
產能擴張受限且 HBM 消耗量極大的雙重壓力下，傳統 DRAM 的供給空間將被進一步擠壓，這
將支撐高階記憶體市場的價格韌性，並促使產能轉向更高毛利的 AI 相關應用。

### Page 12

11
AI Server消耗大量DRAM產能
資料來源： Trendforce、兆豐投顧整理
2027 年AI 晶片對 HBM的需求量不斷攀升，進而大量消耗記憶體原廠產能。由於單顆 GPU 搭
載的 HBM 容量不斷攀升，加上 HBM 的層數增加與生產良率限制，使得每生產一組 AI 系統就
需要投入極高比例的晶圓產能，尤其RUBIN及TPU系列之伺服器需求龐大。
原預估AI Server所消耗原廠DRAM高階產能達近70%，然產能不足導致NVIDIA採取降規，預估
達高階產能約50%。

全球 NAND 市場高度集中於前六大原廠，呈現典型的寡佔格局，而龍頭三星（29.3%）與 SK

### Page 15

14
NAND FLASH供需比週期性循環
NAND FLASH市場呈現明顯的週期性循環，每約3.5～4年進入一個完整供需週期，過往NAND
FLASH市場都相較穩定，然而AI快速發展下帶動儲存供需缺口擴大，預期2027年HDD需求持續
強勁且eSSD滲透率提升下，將持續供不應求態勢。
資料來源：Gartner、Trendforce 、兆豐投顧整理
NAND FLASH供需比
推論需求擴大，帶動儲存市場規模提升

### Page 16

15
NAND FLASH價格維持高檔
自 2025 年第四季開始TLC、QLC NAND價格開始加速上漲呈現陡峭的上升趨勢，且漲勢預計將
持續攀升，主要受到CSP大廠持續進入市場搶貨，預期AI持續發展下，HDD及eSSD滲透率提升
下供不應求態勢延續，價格將維持高檔。
資料來源：Trendforce，兆豐投顧整理

### Page 17

16
NAND 短期無大規模擴產
大部分NAND原廠於2027年並無明顯擴產計畫，並且生產HBM之廠商如三星及海力士將產能優
先用於生產HBM導致NAND產能有下滑之趨勢，唯有中國廠商YMTC較積極擴產預期2027年擴產
7萬片/月至27萬片/月。

17
AI與伺服器為成長核心
資料來源：Trendforce、兆豐投顧整理
手機 與 企業級 SSD 為目前最大的兩個NAND FLASH終端應用市場，於2026年手機及企業級
SSD分別佔24.5%及44.8%，然2027年消費性市場受到記憶體上漲影響，預期出貨量都將呈現
年減態勢，但企業級 SSD 滲透率將持續走升，預期將會超過5成佔比。

18
伺服器出貨量及平均儲存量雙雙成長
資料來源：Trendforce、兆豐投顧整理
全球伺服器持續成長下，2024 年至 2027 年出貨量逐年走升，AI server及一般型server 需求
同走升下，帶動企業級SSD市場規模提升，除出貨量帶動NAND 需求走升外，隨伺服器持續迭
代平均每伺服器所搭載之儲存也持續升級，帶來更多需求。

### Page 20

19
推論需求帶動NAND成長
資料來源：Gartner、 Trendforce、兆豐投顧彙整
Nvidia ICMS (Inference Context Memory Storage) 的快取層級架構，用於解決大型語言模型
推論時的記憶體瓶頸，推升NAND需求，提升Token吞吐量5 倍、推論延遲顯著下降、能效優化
5 倍。
推論需求擴大，帶動儲存市場規模提升

### Page 21

20
NAND製程發展趨勢
資料來源：兆豐投顧整理
目前主流製程為 176 層～238 層，其中 Samsung V8、Micron G8、Kioxia BiCS8、SK hynix
V8 是企業級 SSD 常見使用製程。
雖2025~2026 年起將正式跨入 300 層以上世代（3xxL、V9/V10），儲存密度、效能與功耗管
理將同步優化，V8進入V9世代可透過舊設備更新生產 ，然而進入V10世代則須購買新機台 ，對
於NAND原廠資本支出較有壓力因此普及仍需時間。
推論需求擴大，帶動儲存市場規模提升

南亞科(2408)
推薦個股
投資評等：買進
目標價：800元
推薦重點
展望 2026~2027 年 ， 受惠 AI 需 求 持 續 強 勁 三 大 原 廠 於
DRAM產能持續滿載，預期DDR5、DDR4、DDR3等產品
將持續供不應求 。1C產品預期於27Q1開始驗證，有利打
入AI伺服器供應鏈，預期AI占比將逐季提高。因此兆豐預
估營收 4208.9/6993.1億元 ，YoY+532.1/66.2%，毛利率
83.3/86.3%，EPS為77.85/133.2元。

價持續走升，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價 800元(6 x

### Page 24

24
華邦電(2344)
推薦個股
投資評等：買進
目標價：300元
推薦重點
收購英飛凌旗下 NOR Flash 與 F-RAM事業部，以全球市
佔率來看華邦約23%，英飛凌約11%，使華邦電於全球市
佔率超過30%，加強市場訂價權。
因應客戶強勁需求 ，擴大2026~2027資本支出近400億，
用以擴充台中廠FLASH及高雄廠之DRAM產能，目前高雄
廠產能為 15K/月，預期至 27年擴大至 24~25K/月，其中
16~17K/月產能將以 16nm製程生產為主 ，有利於公司量
產位元數成長一倍，有利於提升公司未來營運動能 。預估
2026/2027年稅後 EPS27.34/61.23元。目標價  300元(5x
27(F)PER) 。

市場焦點
美國
• 9月CPI年增3.7%、PPI年增6.3%，雙雙高於前期 ，能源價格的急升是通膨回升的主要推力 。
雖然核心CPI年增率僅升至2.6%，但住房與服務價格仍維持黏著，使通膨降溫過程出現反覆；
同時PPI增幅明顯高於 CPI，反映企業成本壓力偏高 ，未來不排除進一步向終端價格傳導 。整
體而言，CPI與PPI同步回升反映通膨降溫進程仍存在不確定性 ，進而令市場重估通膨回落速
度與對Fed貨幣政策路徑的審慎預期。
AI
• AI雲端業者 CoreWeave的5年期CDS利差一度突破 1,140個基點 ，旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近
15%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延至股票市場 。全球最大半導體設備商ASML表示，客戶已開始洽
談2029至2030年的訂單；晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期 。與此同時，台灣出口增

速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季
相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。
中國
• 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力
仍在，彭博表示以GDP平減指數衡量 ，經歷三年下滑後 ，整體經濟預計將在 2026年走出通縮 ，
投資上短期可聚焦，受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。

美股獲利引擎轉向半導體/核心AI與能源
• 雖然標普500指數在2026年第三季展現出獲利韌性 ，但隨著油價上漲侵蝕利潤率 、消費者受通膨壓抑 ，以及分析師下調未
來預測動能，市場未來的容錯空間正在縮小。
• AI與半導體仍為獲利領頭羊：科技與半導體持續為標普500指數獲利成長的核心動力，其中約20家企業組成的核心AI一籃子
股票(權重約34.6%)對指數獲利的貢獻度已超過科技七雄(Mag 7)，且此優勢預計將至少延續至2028年底。
• 油價推升能源產業，接棒成為強棒：受到近期國際油價上揚影響，能源產業獲利預測顯著上修，預計獲利成長勁道將延續至
2027年初。在接下來的三個季度中，能源板塊的收益成長表現預計將超越科技七雄。
• 消費與運輸業面臨成本壓頂：相較於科技與能源的強勁，必需消費品與交通運輸業正承受顯著壓力。例如，達美航空受高燃

料成本拖累，而Nike與百事可樂則反映出消費者因持續通膨而縮減支出的困境。
7月以來EPS和營收成長預期修正
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.16
剔除能源、科技七雄和AI一籃子股票後的指數收益成長

顧
利潤率高檔盤整、關稅退款帶來短補，但動能已見放緩
• 利潤率高檔盤整，淨利潤率提升受限：標普500成分股第二季營運利潤率(18.7%)與淨利潤率(15.6%)均創下新高。雖然第三
季營運利潤率預估可進一步提升至19.4%，但淨利潤率預計微幅上升至15.8%，顯示整體企業淨利潤的擴張動能正漸趨平緩 。
• 700億美元關稅退款提供短線補藥： 受惠於美國政府依據國際緊急經濟權力法 (IEEPA)的追償退稅，估計仍有至少700億美元
的關稅款將退還給標普 500成分股(主要集中於工業、非必需消費及醫療保健等 )，這預計能為2026年標普500指數每股收益
(EPS)成長帶來約235個基點、利潤率提升約34個基點的短期提振。
• 上修動能開始轉弱：雖然整體財報指引動量仍維持正值，但過去六週內，分析師對未來12個月EPS與收入的修正動能出現頻

繁下修，這意味著市場先前的極度樂觀情緒正在消退，未來財報季度要優於預期的門檻正在提高。
• 小結與投資建議： 標普500指數短期內靠著半導體 /AI與能源雙引擎拉抬 ，加上關稅退款的一次性利多注入 ，下半年整體獲
利依然具備支撐。然而，高油價帶來的營運成本上升 ，以及終端消費力道的疲軟 ，正逐漸侵蝕非科技/非能源企業的利潤 ，
而大盤廣度不足(僅少數板塊支撐獲利)，代表未來投資風險正逐漸集中 ，但AI算力需求仍屬硬性需求，但波動加劇下，投資
建議以定期定額方式投資美股核心AI與半導體龍頭股票。
利潤率預測
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.16
各產業第二季度收到的關稅退款
未來12月EPS預期修正動能與營收

美國9月CPI、PPI 同步升溫，能源衝擊擴大通膨與利率風險
• 能源價格急升，消費通膨再升溫：美國9月CPI月增0.6%、
年增3.7%，高於前月 3.4%，其中汽油月增 9.1%、能源
價格月增 5.2%，汽油單項即貢獻整體 CPI當月漲幅逾半；
另一方面，核心CPI月增0.3%、年增2.6%、住房指數月
增0.4%，顯示能源雖是本次通膨回升主因 ，但住房及部
分服務價格仍具黏著性，令通膨降溫進程面臨反覆。
• 上游價格大幅攀升 ，成本傳導風險升高： 9月PPI最終需
求月增1.3%、年增6.3%，創2022年3月以來最大增幅，
其中最終需求商品月增 2.6%，能源價格大漲 10.8%，汽
油價格更跳升 20.3%；核心PPI月增0.5%、年增5.0%，
顯示價格壓力不僅來自能源 ，也涉及部分商品與服務環

節；後續是否進一步推升至 CPI，仍須觀察企業成本轉
嫁程度及需求變化。
• 市場看法： 通膨回升恐提高 Fed再度升息機率 ，並延長
高利率維持時間 ，甚至不排除進一步緊縮的可能 ，短期
有利支撐美債殖利率及美元表現 ，不過股、債市後續走
勢仍將受到貨幣政策預期與風險情緒變化影響 。資產配
置建議可關注具成本轉嫁能力 、現金流穩健的能源類股；
債券則宜以短中天期 、高信用評等標的為主 ，以降低利
率敏感度。黃金可作為分散地緣政治及通膨風險的輔助
配置，但不宜將任何單一資產視為必然受惠標的。
CPI 與核心 CPI 走勢變動

供給面推動中國9月CPI升0.9%
• 供給面推動中國9月通膨復甦：中國9月通膨數據顯示，CPI年增率由8月的0.8%升至0.9%，連續兩個月擴大，但低於市場預期
的1.0%，物價上漲主要受到能源及旅遊價格帶動，而食品價格跌幅進一步擴大，使整體通膨壓力仍相對溫和，扣除食品與能源
後，核心CPI維持在1.0%，反映內需需求尚未明顯升溫 。另一方面，PPI年增率由3.8%大幅升至5.0%，優於市場預期，且月增
率達1.2%，創4月以來最大漲幅，顯示上游原物料及工業品價格回升，須關注未來是否逐步傳導至消費端價格。
• 展望後市： 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求 ，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力仍 在，彭博表示 以
GDP平減指數衡量，經歷三年下滑後，整體經濟預計將在2026年走出通縮，值得注意的是，這種供給面驅動的通膨復甦，將降

低官方推出需求刺激措施的緊迫感 ，而要反轉通縮循環正需要官方舉措，投資上短期可聚焦受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的
半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.14
通膨集中於能源和AI相關行業

過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數
的兩倍
• 過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數的兩倍：主因為獲利帶動成長，整體而言，新興市場企
業獲利表現強勁 ，而且仍持續加速成長 ，高盛預估2026年新興市場企業盈餘成長率將達 55%，超過標普500企業盈餘成長速
度的兩倍，且新興市場股票估值較美國股市存在超過40%的折價空間，評價相對具吸引力，今年以來流入新興市場股市的資金
已接近500億美元，創下過去20年來最強勁紀錄之一。更重要的是新興市場國家目前貢獻了全球近 80%的AI硬體出口。隨著AI
產業持續發展，受惠範圍有望從少數龍頭企業擴大至更多國家及公司，特別是那些能夠協助解決供應鏈瓶頸的企業。
• 結論：後市來看在AI產業趨勢、相對低估的評價水準，以及強勁企業獲利成長三大因素支撐下，新興市場股票未來一年仍具備

進一步上漲的潛力，有機會延續目前的強勢表現。

AI投資支撐美國成長，融資需求升高使長端利率與信用風險並存
• AI基礎建設持續擴張 ，成為企業投資重要動能 ：大型雲端業者
持續投入資料中心 、半導體、網路設備與電力基礎設施 ，帶動
相關產業資本支出與供應鏈需求 。摩根大通資產管理指出 ，納
入資料中心開發商及半導體投資後，AI 基礎建設至 2030 年的
累計投資需求可能達約 5兆美元 ，顯示AI投資循環仍具規模 ，
但實際經濟貢獻與企業獲利 ，仍取決於投資效率及商業化成果 。
• 外部融資需求增加 ，債券供給與投資回報成關鍵： 隨AI投資規
模擴大，大型科技企業逐步增加公司債與外部融資 ，在企業債
與公債供給同步增加下 ，長天期利率面臨上行壓力 。若未來AI
獲利成長未能跟上資本支出擴張速度 ，可能壓縮自由現金流與

資本報酬率，進一步擴大信用利差 ，並提高高估值科技股的評
價修正風險
• 市場觀察：AI基礎建設仍是未來數年推動全球科技投資與企業
數位轉型的成長主軸，相關需求具備中長期發展空間，而2026
年市場已逐漸轉向檢視投資回報率 、現金流創造能力及融資結
構是否具備可持續性 ，因此單純依賴資本支出擴張已不足以支
撐所有AI概念股評價 。建議配置可兼顧具獲利與現金流支撐的
AI供應鏈，以及高信用評等 、中短天期債券；對長天期債券則
須評估供給與利率風險 ，並審慎檢視高負債 AI企業的償債能力
及投資回報。
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，永豐金證券， 2026.10.12
AI投資持續挹注美國經濟，對名目GDP維持正面貢獻
資料來源：Morgan Stanley， Bloomberg，永豐金證券， 2026.10.12

加劇固定收益市場資金競爭

AI熱潮、高債務與網路威脅，IMF示警三大金融穩定風險升高
資料來源：Bloomberg Finance L.P， Dealogic，Zero day Clock and IMF staff calculations.， 2026.10.13
AI相關風險持續升溫 主權債券殖利率攀升 網路資安威脅升高
大型雲端科技企業債券發行規模
（占投資等級公司債市場比重) 10年期公債殖利率平均變動幅度 零時差漏洞占比（%）
• AI估值高度集中，市場重定價風險升溫：IMF於10月《全球金融穩定報告》指出，AI相關投資已成為全球成長的重要支撐，
但資金與漲勢高度集中於少數企業及市場，一旦獲利成長不如預期或估值遭重新檢視，可能引發科技股修正並透過風險偏好
下降擴散至整體金融市場。
• 通膨與財政疑慮恐推升債券市場波動： IMF認為，若能源價格推升通膨壓力或市場對財政永續性產生疑慮 ，主權債殖利率可

能進一步上升，進而提高全球融資成本與資產重估風險；在高利率與高債務環境下，金融條件快速緊縮的情況將明顯提高。
• 網路安全風險成為潛在威脅： 隨金融體系數位化程度提高 ，IMF將大型網路攻擊列為重要風險來源之一 。若攻擊事件波及支
付系統、金融基礎設施或雲端服務平台，不僅可能影響市場運作效率，也可能透過信心衝擊與流動性壓力擴大金融市場波動。
• 投資建議：市場成長動能仍受AI支撐，但在金融穩定風險上升情境下，建議採取「AI受惠股＋高品質債券＋防禦型資產」的
槓鈴策略；核心配置可聚焦全球AI基礎建設供應鏈及投資等級債，藉由企業長期獲利成長與債券避險功能，同時因應估值修
正及金融條件收緊風險。

AI隱憂浮現但動能仍在
• CoreWeave信用風險亮紅燈：市場憂心雲端業者在算力租賃業務產生足夠回報前，恐難以負擔龐大債務，導致信用風險升
溫。上週，AI雲端業者CoreWeave的5年期信用違約交換（CDS）利差一度突破1,140個基點，升至通常反映嚴重信用壓力
的水準；旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近15%，其中2032年到期的無擔保債券於上週三跌破面值的80%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延
至股票市場。
• AI半導體產業的基本面仍具韌性：全球最大半導體設備商艾司摩爾（ASML）表示，客戶已開始洽談2029至2030年的訂單；
晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期。與此同時，台灣出口增速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯
示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。

美銀美林調查指出，全球經理人對景氣與股市仍維持偏多立場
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
• 經理人樂觀情緒維持高檔： 美銀美林10月全球基金經理人調查顯示 ，市場風險偏好仍高 ，平均現金水位維持在 3.9%的低檔，
科技股配置則升至2020年8月以來最高，反映經理人對AI產業及企業獲利前景仍具信心 。然而，整體經濟成長預期已轉趨保
守，淨4%的受訪者預期未來 12個月經濟成長放緩 。極端風險方面，債券殖利率無序攀升仍居首位 ，占比31%；AI泡沫及通
膨再起分別為23%及20%。此外，39%的經理人認為，大型雲端業者資本支出是系統性信用事件的主要潛在來源 ，顯示市場
焦點已從AI需求成長延伸至龐大資本支出的獲利回收能力。

• 美國公債殖利率走高 ，債券的收益吸引力逐漸回升： 調查顯示，37%的受訪者表示，當美國10年期公債殖利率升至 6%時，
才會考慮由股票轉向債券；另有19%認為目前殖利率已具吸引力，顯示部分經理人已開始重新評估固定收益資產的配置價值 。
不過，債券整體仍處淨減碼，且經理人對科技股的配置持續升高 ，反映市場尚未全面轉向防禦。後續仍須關注美債殖利率變
化、AI資本支出的投資回報，以及企業獲利能否支撐偏高的市場評價。
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
基金經理人現金水位 最可能影響市場表現的極端風險

台積電財報佳卻未激勵股價，短線面臨總經干擾
• 台積電Q3營收、獲利及EPS同步改寫單季新高：營收年增50.9%、季增17.6%，稅後純益7,630.6億元，年增68.7％、季增8
％；每股稅後純益(EPS)29.43元，整體優於市場預期，但毛利率66.4％較第二季的67.7％下降。
• 財測展望：第四季財測營收預估 523~543億美元，季增上看15％、年增65％，毛利率預估65%~67%，反映高階晶片需求
依然強勁。全年展望方面，預期2026年美元營收年增約44%，較上季預估的「略高於40%」說法更加明確。此外，重申全
年資本支出600~640億美元不變。
• 市場反應：台積電法說會隔日股價小幅下跌，法說會未出現激勵行情，主要原因包含美國通膨升溫壓力、外資連續調節部位，

以及毛利率略為下降。短線籌碼面可能仍會調整，但台積電董事長魏哲家在法說會中強調，第二波AI需求正強勁興起，台積
電正透過多種途徑全力擴充產能，顯示高階晶片前景明確，中長期具投資價值，多家投資機構也紛紛上調目標價。
資料來源：台積電法說會，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.15。
法說會重點整理 說明
AI需求持續升溫
魏哲家坦言，目前仍難以判斷晶圓供需缺口將擴大或縮小，因為
客戶需求持續增加，台積電也正積極提高產能。儘管消費性電子
市場仍受到零組件漲價及總體經濟不確定性影響，但先進製程需
求依然強勁，公司預估2026年全年美元營收將年增約44％。
擴廠進度
(1)台南科學園區新建3奈米廠預計2027年下半年量產，並決定在
台南再增建一座3奈米廠，規畫2028年投入量產。
(2)美國亞利桑那州第二座晶圓廠已完成廠房興建，預計2027年
第三季量產，時程較原先規畫略為提前；日本第二座晶圓廠則將
導入3奈米製程，預計2028年量產。
台積電證實洽談Terafab
合作
魏哲家強調，目前相關合作仍在討論階段，尚未有結論。不過，

無論未來採取何種合作模式，晶圓廠都必須由台積電負責營運。
電力供應疑慮
美國電力供應情況是公司評估AI長期需求的重要因素之一，因此
台積電進一步確認當地電力供應狀況，目前相關研究及評估結果
均良好，尚未發現明顯問題。

• 全球經濟正處於「韌性仍在、風險升高」的階段：國際貨幣基金組織(IMF)指出，AI投資熱潮持續推動美國、台灣等國家成
長，但中東地緣政治衝突、高通膨風險、政府債務問題與AI泡沫化疑慮，也同時為市場埋下不確定因素。
• 市場永遠充滿雜音，定期定額紀律投資更具優勢：本許多人認為定期定額的核心在於「平均成本」，但更重要的是幫助投資
人克服人性。市場上漲時持續投入，可以參與企業獲利成長帶來的成果，市場下跌時持續扣款，則能以較低價格累積更多單
位數。換句話說，定期定額讓投資人不需要每天判斷：明天會不會跌？現在是不是太貴？要不要先停扣觀望？長期而言，紀

律投資往往比短期擇時的成效更佳。
• AI時代更適合定期定額：儘管IMF認為人工智慧發展為全球經濟成長帶來貢獻，但同時也警告，若市場對AI未來獲利能力過
度樂觀，不排除出現股市估值修正。根據歷史經驗，每一次市場重大修正往往也孕育下一輪成長契機，不論AI發展是否出現
泡沫，善用定期定額策略可以在機會與風險之間達到平衡。
韌性仍在、風險升溫，紀律投資更穩健
任一月份定期定額進場投資試算
註：以MSCI全球指數2010/1/31~2025/12/31試算。假設定期定額每月月底投入、
原幣計算。指數資料試算僅供參考，非投資商品之實際績效。資料來源Bloomberg。
短進短出錯失行情的代價大
註：以美國標普500指數為試算範例，期間為1980年~2025年。資料來源Bloomberg。

營 運 分 析

旺宏為全球 NOR Flash領導廠商 
旺宏是全球領先的 NOR Flash 製造商，提供涵蓋廣泛規格和容量的ROM唯
讀記憶體、NOR型及 NAND型快閃記憶體解決方案。目前公司的產能包括8
吋晶圓產能為每月 45-48 千片，12 吋晶圓產能為每月 20 千片，而 ROM 主
要透過外購再進行加工。公司還提供ROM、NOR型快閃記憶體及 NAND型
快閃記憶體的解決方案，並從事次微米邏輯製程、高壓 CMOS 及 BCD製程
的晶圓代工服務，包括嵌入式非揮發性記憶體的 BCD 及邏輯製程。公司的
廠房位於新竹科學園區，包含生產8吋晶圓的晶圓二廠、12吋晶圓的晶圓五
廠，以及產品測試廠。26Q3產品組合：NAND 47%、NOR 46%、ROM 5%、
FBG 2%。

圖三、26Q4產品漲價有利毛利提升 
旺宏各季營收及獲利走勢預估(單位：百萬)

資料來源：旺宏；兆豐投顧預估

NOR Flash 需求穩步上升，車用與伺服器市場成為成長主力 
整體 NOR Flash 產品將持續成長，特別是在車用和伺服器應用方面。隨著汽
車電子化和自動駕駛技術的發展，汽車行業對高可靠性、耐高溫的半導體需
求不斷增加，有利 NOR Flash 在車用市場用量持續增加。此外，伺服器市場
的需求也隨著數據中心的擴展而逐步上升。NOR Flash的快速響應和高可靠
性特性使其非常適合用於伺服器中的關鍵啟動代碼和固件應用。預期旺宏車
規 eMMC 預期於 2027 年開始小量出貨，2028 年放量，增添公司營運動能。

eMMC需求相對強勁，成獲利回溫關鍵 
展望 2026-2027 年，受惠原廠減產趨勢導致 eMMC 需求強勁，車規 eMMC
預期於 2027 年開始小量出貨，2028 年放量，增添公司營運動能，且 NOR 
FLASH 受惠 AI 帶動，需求強勁然後續價格漲幅有限，因應 NAND 及 NOR 
FLASH客戶強勁需求，預期 27H2 年新產能開出，將再增強公司擴力表現。
因此兆豐預估 2026/2027 營收 818.97/1229.03 億元，YoY+183.6/50.1%，
毛利率 66.7/74.1%，稅後淨利 342.1/582.8 億元，EPS 為 17.25/29.39 元。

記憶體持續供不應求，然漲價力道放緩，調降為「逢低買進」投資評等 
旺宏目前位於 3.5X 27(F)PER)，基於 eMMC 及 NOR強勁需求，然漲價力道
有限，調降為「逢低買進」投資評等，目標價 120 元(4.1 x 2 7(F)PER)，潛
在上漲空間 16.5%。

長線來看，車用與伺
服器應用成為 NOR 
Flash成長動能 
漲價力道有限，調降
為「逢低買進」投資
評等,目標價 120元。

4Q26/2027 年獲利將續創新高，受惠 DDR4 供不應求延續 
預估 4Q26 營收季增 13.2%至 1,512.46 億 元 ， 其 中 預 期ASP 再 季 增
15~20%、雖收斂但仍維持雙位數季增；毛利率季增 0.4ppts 至 86.1%，報
價維持高檔下與 3Q26 相近；EPS 季增 11.9%至 28.42 元。展望後續，月產
能未來四年內將由目前約 65kwpm 分階段新增 35.9kwpm 的產能、2H27 開
始貢獻，供需緊俏延續下 ASP 將維持高檔，4Q26/2027 年營運續創新高。 
2027 年 DRAM 供需緊俏與報價上行持續，主要由 Server 驅動 
本中心預期 2027 年 DRAM 持續供不應求，報價仍在上行循環，最主要驅動
力 Server 於整體位元需求佔比過半且供需差達 20%以上(高於整體 DRAM
的約 10%)，其中預期 2027 年 HBM 市場規模年增 243%，Nvidia VR200 搭
載 SOCAMM2 位元需求成長達 6 倍，General Server 亦佔整體 Server 需求
一半比重，相關 RDIMM、LPDRAM 成長強勁；同時新增產能相對需求成長
仍有限，三大原廠 2027 年底 DRAM 投片月產能僅年增 10~15%，中國同業
長鑫存儲雖擴產積極仍僅佔整體產能約 15%，主要供應中國國內需求。此外，
DDR4 於消費性/網通/Server 仍具龐大需求，目前 AI Server 仍有約 10%週
邊應用搭載 DDR4，供給則在國際原廠 EOL 下大幅縮減，長鑫存儲重心亦
已轉往 DDR5，預估 2027 年 DDR4 仍有 20%以上的供需缺口(詳見圖 7)。 
產品組合持續往 Server/AI 提升，中長期營運展望正向 
綜 合 上 述 ， 預 估2026/27 年 營 收 年 增 525.5%/66.8% 至 4,165.31 億元
/6,946.17 億元 ，毛利率年增 60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5% ，EPS 年增
3457.6%/68.6%至 75.93/128.01 元。考量 1) 公司現為 DDR4 第一大廠且同
時具備 DDR5 產品布局、2) 產品組合持續往 Server/AI 提升、3) WoW 客製
化產品有望帶來更穩定獲利支撐，中長期營運更加穩健，以 2.5 倍 PB(過去
三年落在 0.4~2.8 倍)、2027 年 BVPS 2 30.7 元，給予買進、目標價 570 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
4Q26 預期 ASP 再季增 15~20%，2027 年 DDR4 供不應求延續下合約
價仍維持高檔，獲利將年增 68.6%續創新高。 
⧫  
2027 年 DRAM 產業 供 需 緊 俏 及 報 價 上 行 持 續 ， 最 主 要 驅 動 力 為
Server 相關 DRAM 訂單需求大幅成長，新增產能則相對有限。 
⧫  
產品組合續往 Server/AI 提升，中長期營運更加穩健，給予買進、目標
價 570 元(2.5x 2027 年 BVPS)。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第2 頁，共 20頁
營運分析
3Q26 獲利季增 74.6%，主因 ASP 季增>60%
3Q26 營收 1,336.49 億元，季增 61.9%/年增 611.7%，其中 1) 平均售價(ASP)：季增超過 60%/年
增超過 500%， 受 惠 半 年 期/一年期等 LTA 重 新 議 價 及 季 度 合 約 價 調 整 、2) 位 元 出 貨 量(Bit
shipment)：季減中個位數百分比 /年減低個位數百分比，主因新廠產能尚未開出且庫存處於低水位；
毛利率達 85.7%，季增 6.2ppts/年增 67.2ppts，受惠 ASP 大幅成長；營業利益率 80%，季增
6.3ppts/年增 74ppts，費用方面提列當期及未來之員工薪酬增加；稅後獲利 876.27 億元，季增
74.6%/年增 5505.1%；EPS 25.40 元，整體 3Q26 獲利大致符合市場預期。

供不應求延續下，2027 年 DRAM 合約價仍維持高檔且獲利續創新高
本次法說會重點包括：1) 公司表示 DDR4 市場環境健康，DDR4 目前佔整體 DRAM 市場需求仍有
約 12~14%，雖需求逐漸縮小但對應大幅縮減的供給仍相當強勁，ASP 仍有望保持雙位數成長；2)
2026 年資本支出指引不超過新台幣 697 億元，2026~2029 年 5A 新廠資本支出 預算約新台幣
3,466 億元，屏東新廠長期資本支出規劃達 100 億美元，主要用於 AI 應用的 WoW 3D 晶圓封裝與

測試需求，預計 2H28 起設備裝機； 3) Server/AI 相關目前營收占比 >20%，公司指引將成長至
2029~2030 年的 40~50%，預期隨新產能上線持續提升。
展望 2026/2027 年，目前月產能推估約 65kwpm，而 5A 新廠具備約 50kwpm 的擴充空間，目前規
劃 2028 年將投產至 30kwpm，新增產能預計於 2H27 開始微幅貢獻，相關資本支出預算約新台幣
3,466 億元(含 EUV 設備)，長期而言公司亦預計於屏東科學園區投資設立 3D 晶圓封裝測試廠，導
入矽穿孔(TSV)、晶圓堆疊等 3DIC 先進技術；位元出貨量方面，2026 年在晶圓產出尚待新廠產能
開出、以生產效率提升與 16nm 製程微幅增加貢獻下，公司維持年增 high-10s % 的指引，2027 年
考量高基期且新廠產能於下半年才開始貢獻，預估 2H27 前位元出貨量維持平穩。
ASP 則受惠市場供需緊俏，1~3Q26 每季皆季增約 60~70%，4Q26 估亦將維持 15~20%的雙位數

季增幅；進入 2027 年，DDR4 供不應求態勢延續，但考量報價已處高檔、非 AI 下游終端漸難以負
荷，且記憶體原廠毛利率多已達 80%以上，預估 2027 年各季 ASP 持平至季增個位數百分比。
綜合上述，預估 2026/2027 年營收年增 525.5%/66.8%至 4,165.31 億元/6,946.17 億元；毛利率年
增 60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5%，2026 年毛利率受惠 ASP 大幅年增已顯著提升至高水位，2027
年報價上行趨勢持續但逐漸持穩於高檔，折舊方面， 2026 年公司預估資本支出以不超過 新台幣
697 億元為上限，其中約 50%用於設備採購，2027 年隨 5A 新廠投產所需設備投資持續增加，資
本支出將大幅年增，故在 2026 年隨先前廠房設備折舊陸續到期使折舊成本相對低之後，2027 年折
舊金額將明顯增加，但在目前 ASP 與獲利皆維持高檔且加計新產出貢獻下，整體 2027 年毛利率受
折舊影響可控；營益率年增 68.8ppts/2.2ppts 至 76.7%/78.9%；稅後獲利年增 3861%/68.6%至

2,619.4 億元/4,416.29 億元；EPS 75.93/128.01 元。
除報價與產能拉升外，中長期可關注趨勢包括： 1) Server/AI 相關應用占比持續提升、 2) DDR5
16Gb 6400/7200Mbps 及以上貢獻增加、3) WoW 客製化高頻寬記憶體放量。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第5 頁，共 20頁  
產品結構分析 
受惠 DDR4 供需緊俏及寡佔優勢、Server/AI 占比續增，亦具備 DDR5 產品布局 
雖三大國際原廠 DDR4 EOL 以將產能轉向 HBM/DDR5，中國同業長鑫存儲(CXMT)亦將重心轉往
DDR5，但 DDR4 於消費性/網通/Server 等仍具龐大需求，在供給大幅縮減下，公司目前已為全球
第一大 DDR4 供應商，高度寡占且具備價格主導權，除 8Gb DDR4 以外，16Gb 亦於 2H26 開始貢
獻營收。公司目前 Server/AI 相關應用營收比重已超過 20%，且占比將隨新產能開出而提升，持續
受惠 Storage、BMC、NIC、Switch 應用等貢獻，相較國內同業有望減緩消費性疲弱影響。 
DDR4 為公司目前產業地位、獲利最有利的產品組合，但其亦具備 DDR5 技術能力與相關營收貢獻，
目前採 1B 製程的 16Gb DDR5 已量產出貨，傳輸速率現以 6400Mbps 為主並持續開發 7200Mbps
及以上規格。未來若終端客戶需求加速由 DDR4 轉向 DDR5，公司得以靈活調整產能配置以增加出
貨，持續拿下市場份額。除一般型 DRAM 以外，公司亦有 LPDDR4 產品出貨，LPDDR5 則有望於
2027 年導入量產。 
圖 8：目前營收仍以 DDR4 為主

圖 9：1B/1C 營收占比持續提升

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
WoW 客製化高頻寬記憶體為長期成長利基，布局 Edge AI 與外溢市場需求 
WoW(Wafer-on-Wafer)客製化高頻寬記憶體為有利公司中長期營運體質的重要成長動能。目前的標
準 HBM 為 IC 對 IC 堆疊，主要用於 Cloud AI 訓練等市場，然而 Edge 端裝置受限於功耗、成本與
物理體積等限制無法直接採用，同時三大國際原廠有限之 HBM 產能亦無法完全滿足整體 Cloud 端
市場需求，公司亦將受惠此外溢效果。其客製化產品採用 Wafer-on-Wafer Hybrid Bonding( 晶圓級
混合鍵合)技術，將 DRAM 與邏輯 Core Die 直接對接堆疊，能以顯著之成本與功耗優勢滿足客戶於
Edge 端的高頻寬需求。目前有多個客戶專案進行中，預估 2H27~2028 年可見較清晰的量產營收，
新廠產能規劃上亦保留彈性予 WoW 客製化 DRAM 專案，長線有望為公司提供較穩定的獲利支撐。 
圖 10：Wafer-on-wafer 堆疊技術示意圖

資料來源：Samsung、元大投顧整理

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第6 頁，共 20頁  
財務分析 
整體財務結構於產業上行及下行循環期間皆維持穩健 
公司在 2020~2022 年 上 行 循 環 期 間 ， 流 動 比 率 與 速 動 比 率 分 別 高 達2020/2021/2022 年的
874%/678%/652%及 707%/599%/502%，後續下行期間雖降至 2023/2024 年的 480%/329%及
339%/217%，但整體仍位於健康水準，且負債比率在上行及下行期間均維持在低於 20%的低水準，
短長期償債能力無虞。2025 年以來隨著記憶體市況轉佳、報價顯著上漲，加上 1H26 完成 787.18
億元之私募案，流動比率與速動比率再回升至 2Q26 的 691%與 635%，負債比率則為 17%，預期
後續持續優化。 
經營能力方面，應收/應付帳款週轉天數落在 36~60 天/19~48 天的健康區間，且兩者差距在合理範
圍內。然而，存貨週轉天數受記憶體景氣循環影響，由上行循環期間 2020/2021 年的 130/97 天，
攀升至下行循環期間 2023/2024 年的 271/333 天，使整體現金轉換週期由 2021 年的 120 天拉長至
2024 年 337 天，反映景氣谷底期間庫存積壓對營運資金運轉效率帶來短期壓力。不過隨著 2025
年以來 DRAM 報價顯著上漲且公司庫存去化加速，2Q26 存貨週轉天數已降至 124 天，帶動現金轉
換週期縮短至 118 天，預期將持續改善。 
觀察現金流與資本支出動向，公司於 2021 年達到 328.47 億元的自由現金流高峰，隨後於
2022~2024 年下行期間，公司雖降低但仍投入 100 多億元的資本支出以推動製程技術組合轉往
10nm 級發展，自由現金流 2023/2024 年轉為淨流出 193.4/141.91 億元。儘管自由現金流短暫承壓，
公司在手現金仍穩健維持於 500 億元以上之充沛水準， 2025 年進入上行循環後營運顯著轉佳，
1H26 自由現金流已達 805.52 億元，加上引進 Kioxia、SanDisk、Solidigm 及 Cisco 之高達 787.18
億元的私募案挹注，使 2Q26 底在手現金大幅提升至 2,176.03 億元的歷史新高，且隨著營運成長
將持續增加，足以支應 5A 新廠未來四年高達 3,466 億元之資本支出規劃，整體財務結構穩健。

產業概況  
2027 年 DRAM 供需緊俏與報價上行持續，主要由 Server 需求驅動 
本中心預期 2027 年 DRAM 將持續供不應求，報價仍在上行循環，其中最主要驅動力為 Server 相
關 DRAM 訂單需求大幅成長且優於預期，Server 佔整體 DRAM 位元需求比重持續增加，預期自
2026 年的 54%成長至 2027 年的 63%，供需差更達 20%以上，高於整體 DRAM 的約 10%，主因：
1) Server 需求大幅成長，如本中心預估 2026/2027 年 ASIC 晶片量年增 91%/88%；2) Server 單位
DRAM Content 持續提升；3) AI Inference/Agentic AI 帶動 DRAM 儲存需求多樣化，如擴大導入
LPDDR(SOCAMM)於 Server 架構中，其中預估 2027 年 Nvidia VR200 機櫃對應 CPU 搭配的
SOCAMM2 位元需求成長高達 6 倍。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第8 頁，共 20頁  
預期 2027 年 HBM 整體市場規模年增 243%，其中位元出貨量年增 55%、ASP 翻倍成長，主因：1) 
HBM 先前因年度議價機制無法反映 2H25 以來 DRAM 漲勢，近期已重新議定 2027 年度合約價；2) 
HBM4 放量出貨，其中 HBM4 佔整體 HBM 產值比重將達 46%。此外，雖 Rubin Ultra 、TPU v9 搭
配的 HBM4E 將由 12Hi 降至 8Hi，但主要為因應供給不足下為確保出貨顆數的應對措施，並非需求
下修。2027 年 HBM 市場份額預期 SK Hynix 約 40~45%、Samsung 約 35~40%、Micron 約
25~30%，SK Hynix 仍為 HBM 龍頭，但因 Samsung HBM4 進度優於同業、SK Hynix 持續調整
HBM4 效能，使 Samsung 市佔率具提升空間。 
圖 21：2027 年 HBM 市場規模將年增 243% 圖 22：HBM 佔整體 DRAM 投片比重持續提升

資料來源：元大投顧預估 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
以 BOM Cost 分布來看，DRAM/NAND 漲價對於 General Server 與 PC 價格之影響程度較高，如
DRAM/NAND 漲 10%，對於 AI Server/ General Server/PC/Smartphone 整機價格之影響分別為
1%/5.3%/3~3.6%/2~3%。 
而組裝廠更改記憶體規格之彈性來看，PC 最高、Server 其次、AI Server 接續、Smartphone 最低。
其中 PC 品牌廠因各地所需之規格不同且主機板可調整彈性相對手機高，加上每年都會針對原有的
零組件對供應商要求降價，因此可以最快調整其記憶體設計以因應記憶體價格變化，因此將較快出
現產品降規之情況；Server 部分以 Storage Server 所受之影響最大，尤其對於以中小企業客戶為
主之白牌業者其價格敏感度較高，不過對 CSP 來說對於 General Server/AI Server( 占比小)漲價之
接受度仍高；而 Smartphone 則只能在下一代產品發布前更改設計，多只能自行吸收或是對下游反
映售價，對於品牌廠毛利與終端需求影響較大。 
圖 23：記憶體漲價對 Storage server/PC/Smartphone 影響較大，AI Server 則相對輕微 
  DRAM % of cost   NAND % of cost   Sensitivity*

等用途；***PC 僅計算 DIMM 與 SSD；****手機僅計算 ROM 與 UFS。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第9 頁，共 20頁  
2027 年原廠 DRAM/NAND 新增產能有限，仍不足以因應強勁成長的需求 
雖 2027 年中部份新產能將上線，三大 DRAM 國際原廠年底投片月產能預估皆僅較 2026 年底增加
10~15%，長鑫存儲擴產幅度雖達 20~30%仍難以滿足中國國內需求 (目前中國 DRAM 自給率僅
30~40%)，即使搭配製程轉換以提升位元產出，仍 無法滿足本中心預估之全球 DRAM 位元需求量
30%以上的強勁成長，其中國際原廠擴產計畫如下： 
1) Samsung ：P4L 自 3Q25 起陸續投產，預計至 2027 年底月產能將達約 150kwpm，主要用於 1c 
DRAM 以因應強勁的 HBM4 需求，並持續推動現有廠房舊製程轉往 1c nm 以滿足 HBM 與通用型
1c DRAM 市場；此外，P5L Fab 1 已於 2025 年底復工、Fab 2 提前至 2026 年 7 月動工，產能目
標高於現有廠房約 50%以上。 
2) SK Hynix ：M15X 2026 下半年預計投產約 40kwpm，2027 年底月產能將達約 80kwpm，主要用
於 1b 並部分導入 1c DRAM ；公司亦表示 Y1 首個無塵室設備裝機將提前 3 個月至 2027 年 5 月，
以因應強勁的 AI 相關記憶體需求，預計至 2030 年上半年分階段開出共 360kwpm 的產能，Y2 則
預計 2027 年 7 月動工、2029 年 6 月投產，用於生產 HBM 及下世代 DRAM。 
3) Micron ：美國 ID1 預計 2027 年中產出第一批晶圓、ID2 則預計 2028 年底產出；向力積電添購
之銅鑼廠預計提前一季至 2027 年中出貨；新加坡廠區 HBM 封裝產能將於 2027 年上半年開始貢獻。 
4) 長鑫存儲：上海新廠 2027 年開始投產，主要針對 HBM3 等高端記憶體產品，亦新建後段封測廠
以填補中國在 TSV 及堆疊封裝的供應鏈空白，合肥 Phase 3 則預計以 LPDDR4X/5/5X 等產品為主，
預計 2028 年開始投產。

獲利預估與股票評價
正向看待南亞科後續營運，目標價 570 元
展望後續，在 ASP 維持高檔且新廠產能將於 2H27 開始逐步貢獻下，正向看待公司中長期營運成
長，預估 2026/2027 年營收年增 525.5%/66.8%至 4,165.31 億元/6,946.17 億元 ，毛利率年增
60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5%，EPS 年增 3457.6%/68.6%至 75.93/128.01 元。
目前公司股價約在 1.8 倍 2027 年每股淨值(BVPS) ( 隱含本益比 3.2 倍)，低於國外同業平均的 2.0
倍，公司過去三年 PB 則落在 0.4~2.8 倍區間。雖目前循環處於 DRAM 報價仍維持高檔但預期後續
季增幅將收斂的階段，然而考量 1) 公司為 DDR4 第一大廠的產業地位且同時具備 DDR5 產品布局、
2) 產品組合將逐步擺脫消費性並持續往 Server/AI 提升(目前相關營收占比已超過 20%)、3) WoW

客製化產品有望帶來更穩定的獲利支撐，有助公司中長期營運更加穩健，以 2.5 倍 PB、2027 年
BVPS 230.7 元，推得目標價 570 元，給予買進評等。

公司簡介  
南亞科技成立於 1995 年，近年於全球 DRAM 市佔率約 1~2%，排名第五，公司目前以 DDR4 為主(佔比 60~70%)，亦具備
DDR5 產品，以應用別而言，PC & Server 約 25~30%、Mobile 約 10~15%、Consumer 約 60~70%。公司生產基地為新北
泰山 3A 廠，月產能 6~6.5 萬片。由於 DDR4/5 為長期主流市場，公司將陸續發展 1B/1C/1D 製程，且預計 2026~2029 年
投入新台幣 3,466 億元建置 5A 新廠產能，預估新產能將於 2H27~2028 年開出。

### Page 17

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第17 頁，共 20頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 南亞科整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在略為領先的位
置，但在半導體設計與製造的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 南亞科的整體曝險屬於中等水準，與半導體設計與製造行業的平均水準相當。公司較需要改善
的 ESG 議題包含商業道德以及人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：南亞科在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬較佳。ESG 報告體系完善，公
司已指定董事會成員負責監督 ESG 相關事宜。使用 ESG 目標評估高階主管績效；然而，這些目標並未在薪酬政策中明
確規定。儘管如此，公司的檢舉人保護計畫和環境政策均較為完善。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

### Page 1

M
Idea
Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
2H26 Driven by Semis and
Photonics; 2027 Seen as
Another Strong Year
A unique combination of power, electrical and optical testing
tool capabilities position the company well in AI deployment.
Stay OW.
We see 3Q26 EPS of NT$13.71: Chroma's 3Q26 revenue came in at NT$17,926mn,
up 32% QoQ and 180% YoY. We believe the strength was driven mainly by the
semiconductor and photonics businesses, while the power testing business likely

remained at an elevated level. Within the semiconductor and photonics businesses,
SL T  should benefit from AI HPC customers' ramping; meanwhile, optical testing
tools for transceivers and, to a lesser extent, CPO, are also seen as growth drivers
during the quarter. We estimate GM of 60.5% (flat QoQ) as scale tailwinds should
be largely offset by a less favorable product mix, while OPM should expand by
0.5ppt QoQ to 39.3% on some operating leverage. Overall, we expect EPS of NT

$13.71 (+13% QoQ/+15% YoY).
Growth momentum seen sustained into 2027: Looking ahead, we expect Chroma
to see another year of strong growth, supported by the power testing (51% growth
for 42% mix) and semi & photonics businesses (58% growth for 40% mix).
• Power testing: The ever-increasing power consumption of AI clusters should
continue to drive demand for higher spec testing tools. Along with our
forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit demand in the next

three years (see report), we think this should stay a major driver for Chroma.
• SLT: We are seeing more HPC chip designers adopting the system level
testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. As the first mover in this market. Chroma should benefit from
increasing chip volume as well as higher TDP, in our view.
• Photonics: Transceivers should continue to be the mainstream solution;
architectures such as CPO and NPO also need testing tools.

• Emerging business: We expect Chroma, by leveraging its existing customer
relationship and technology expertise, to gain more traction in FT handlers
and burn-in ovens for AI HPCs in 2027. These are the markets that Chroma
previously focused less on, so they represent new opportunities.
Stay OW: We remain constructive on Chroma for its testing equipment's unique
combination of power, electrical and optical capabilities, allowing it to capture
growth opportunities in various aspects of AI deployment. Our PT remains NT

$2,800 as our estimate increases are offset by a lower target multiple of 40x 2027e
P/E (previously 45x) to factor in generally more conservative sentiment toward the
AI space now. This implies a PEG of 0.6 (vs. 1.0 over the past decade).

increasing power consumption of AI clusters should continue to drive demand for higher
spec testing tools. Along with our forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit

demand in the coming three years (see our report), this should remain a major driver for
Chroma, in our view.

Multiple drivers in the semiconductor business
System-level testing  (SL T) business
SL T should benefit from the capex cycle of NVIDIA's Rubin generation GPU, AMD's MI450
GPU and more ASIC accelerators.  Management pointed out that discrete testing tools are
still preferred  by major customers.  We are also seeing more HPC chip designers adopting

the system level testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. We believe Chroma, as the first mover in this market, should benefit from
increasing chip volume, as well as higher TDP.
Metrology business
The metrology business should continue to benefit from current advanced packaging
architectures from its foundry and OSAT customers. It is confident that the performance
of its tools can win future architecture projects, which our industry checks suggest should

have higher metrology density.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Exhibit 3: Chroma –  Offerings for Metrology in Advanced Packaging
Source: Chroma
Chroma's metrology equipment employs white light interferometry technology. The basic
working principle is that white light is emitted on the reference mirror and the sample,
which are placed at the same distance from the light source. The reflected beams from
the reference mirror and sample surface are then received by a light sensor.
Interference occurs when two light beams collide, which could cause  them to either

strengthen or weaken, depending on the phase of the two light beams. Thus, by
measuring the intensity of the combined light beams, the distance between light sensor
and multiple points on the target surface can be calculated through an algorithm. This can
then be used to measure the dimensions of the structure or the alignment of layers.
Exhibit 4: Concept of Light Interference
Source: Keyence
Exhibit 5: Setup of Interferometer
Source: Wikipedia
CPO contribution to start from 2026

Chroma has been qualified for optical engine testing in Insertion 3 and system-level
testing in Insertion 4, and there might be incremental opportunities if supply chain players'
progress falls behind for testing equipment in Insertion 2. Over the longer term, Chroma
also  aspires to participate in the PIC optical/electrical/reliability testing in Insertion 1.

Exhibit 6: Chroma's Offerings in CPO
Source: Chroma
FT handlers to add another revenue stream
In HPC testing, Chroma used to focus mostly on the SL T side, although it did unveil its first
HPC FT handler earlier this year and is now going through the qualification process. We
believe this is a natural extension of its expertise and customer relationships accumulated
in SL Ts. We expect Chroma to gain more traction in FT handlers for AI HPCs in 2027.

accounting for 36% of Chroma's mix.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Our price target (base case scenario value) remains  NT$2,800: Our PT is based on a
target multiple of 40x 2027e P/E (vs. 45x 2027e previously).   We lowered our multiple to
factor in recent volatility in market sentiment toward AI.   We view our multiple as justified
based on our estimated 68% operating profit CAGR over 2025-28. This implies a PEG of
0.6 vs. its average of 1.0 in the past decade, when the stock was trading at an average of

~19x forward P/E with an operating profit CAGR of 20%.
Bull case scenario value – NT$3,900 (unchanged): We apply a 55x multiple (vs. 62x P/E
previously)  to our earnings estimate for 2027.  This scenario reflects stronger  5G and HPC
demand, driving more earnings upside.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,100 (unchanged): We apply a 16x P/E multiple (vs. 18x
P/E previously) to our earnings estimate for 2027. This scenario factors in weaker HPC
revenue as hyperscalers start to focus more on ROI for AI capex than they did in the past

couple of years.
Risks:
• Softer-than-expected AI capex
• Slower progress in advanced packaging capacity expansion
• Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

 Update
ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
  CPO Revenue on Track; Stay 
OW 
 CPO is on track for >US$500mn in 2027: Management mentioned the CPO has 
already started mass production  and will reach at least US$500mn in 2027. This is 
in-line with our estimates that we forecast CPO revenue to be >US$600mn in 2027.
Slightly upward revision of LEAP revenue: The company slightly raised 2026 LEAP 
(leading-edge advanced packaging) revenue from US$3.7bn to US$4bn and  2027 
from >US$7.5bn to >US$8bn, on track  to our forecast of US$9bn. It expects at least 
US$1bn of full process CoWoS-like revenue in 2027 vs. MSe of US$1.8bn.
Margin expansion is also on track: 3Q26 ATM gross margin has already surpassed 
the structural range and reached 30.6%. The company mentioned 30% would be 
the new floor and expects it to continue to expand sequentially in 2027.
3Q26 GM and 4Q26 guidance slightly beat: 3Q26 GM of 22.1% is slightly higher 
than both MSe and the consensus. 4Q26 revenue guidance of 6-8% QoQ growth 
and GM of 23-24% are both slightly higher than MSe of 5% QoQ growth and 22-23% 
GM forecast.

STOCK RATINGS

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

2026.10.29   
HD현대중공업  (329180)

3Q26: 조선사는 우직해야 한다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 5조 7,536억원/영업이익 8,992억원 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 매출은 5조 7,536억원/영업이익은 8,992억원으로 
컨센서스(6조 1,295억원/9,671억원) 및 당사 추정치(6조 859억원/9,679억원) 대비 
매출 및 영업이익 각각 -6.1%/-7.0%, -5.5%/-7.1%를 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다.  
조선사업부 매출이 4조 3,988억원/영업이익 6,463억원(OPM 14.7%)이며 이중 상선 
OPM은 14.7%로, 2분기 17.8%에 비해 3.1%P 하락하였으나 1분기 15.9%와 크게 
차이나지 않는 수준을 유지하면서 조업일수 및 환율 하락에도 불구하고 P-MIX및 
빈티지 개선에 따른 효과를 보여주었다. 해양사업부는 매출 3,799억원/영업이익 
1,265억원(OPM 33.3%)로 지난 분기(15.9%)보다도 훨씬 높은 영업이익을 
기록하였다. 엔진기계사업부는 매출 9,511억원/영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)를 
기록하며 역시 견조한 실적을 기록하였다.  
일회성 이익으로 Trion 공사의 C/O 180 억원, 일회성 손실로는 셧다운에 따른 
조업감소 영향 500억원 및 환율하락에 따른 이익 감소분 450억원이 있었으며 금액은 
공개되지 않았으나 임단협 잠정타결에 따른 성과급/타결금 지급에 대한 추가 충당금의 
75% 인식이 반영되었다. 알려진 일회성만 제거하더라도 실질 영업이익률은 
17%이상으로 2분기 16.4%보다 높아진 호실적이라고 할 수 있다. 특히 RUYA 공사가 
본 궤도에 오르면서 해양사업부의 이익률이 괄목할 만큼 높아졌으며 지속가능하다는 
회사의 설명이다.

투자의견 BUY 및 조선업종 TOP PICK 유지, 목표주가 530,000원 하향 
HD현대중공업의 목표주가를 530,000원으로 38.3% 하향하지만, 투자의견 BUY와 
조선업종 TOP PICK 을 유지한다. 환율 하락 및 28년 하반기 엔진증설 매출확대, 
해양공사 이익률 상승 등 요인을 종합 반영하여 실적 추정치를 미세 반영하여 도출한 
2027F BPS 131,910원에 Target P/B를 기존대비 하향한 4.0배로 적용하였다. 특수선 
기대감 및 선가상승 기대감 하락에 따른 조선업종 전반의 밸류에이션 하락을 반영하여 
Target을 낮췄지만 그럼에도 조선업종 중 가장 높은 멀티플을 부여하며 Top 
Pick의견을 견지한다. 여러 악재에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업은 탄탄하다. 본업인 
상선사업부의 9월말 기준 수주량은 137.9억 달러로 목표치를 이미 초과 달성하였으며 
전사 기준으로도 수주목표 90.5%를 채워넣었다. 수주잔고 또한 상선 421억 
달러/전사 609억 달러로 각각 작년 말 대비 12.6%/13.2% 증가하였다. 해양부문과 
엔진기계부문은 조선사업부보다도 높은 이익률로 든든한 실적의 기반이 되고 있다.  
최근 HD현대그룹은 미국의 EPC강자 Kiewit과 미국 합작법인 설립에 관한 계약을 
맺고 그린필드 기반의 미국 신규 조선소 건설을 추진하고 있다. 빠르면 올해 중으로 
건립과 관련한 세부 사항이 공개될 예정이며 HD현대중공업이 중추적인 역할을 담당할 
것으로 보인다. 일찍부터 브라운필드 방식으로 접근한 동종사와 달리 다소 시간은 
걸렸지만 과감하게 결정된 투자다. 조선소 예상 준공 역시 2028년 이후로 예상되어 
당장 기업가치에 반영하기는 어려우나 엔진 증설과 함께 2028년 후에는 분명한 실적 
상향 요인이 될 것으로 예상한다. 우직하게 본업에 충실하며 신중하게 미래를 준비하는 
조선소, 귀감이다.  
Buy (Maintain)

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

Document 234: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

HD현대중공업의 3Q26 연결 실적은 매출액 5조 8,602억원(yoy +33%, qoq -7%), 
영업이익 9,161억원(yoy +64%, qoq -12%, OPM 15.6%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 하회할 
전망이다. 동사의 3Q26 조업일수는 여름휴가, 추석연휴 및 중대재해 등 영향으로 
qoq -21% 감소했으며 환율 역시 qoq -84원 감소한 영향을 감안해 전분기 대비 
실적을 보수적으로 추정했다. 그럼에도 해양 부문 Trion FPU발 C/O에 따른 일회성 
환입이 예상됨에 따라 일부 이익 상쇄가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 한편, 동사의 
임단협 부결로 당분기 추가적인 인건비 부담은 제한적일 것으로 판단되며 잠수함 
화재 사고 관련 비용 역시 4Q 이후 반영될 전망이다.

동사는 급격히 커져가는 AIDC향 전력수요 및 발전용 엔진 수주에 대응하고자 9/10 
총 4GW 규모의 4행정 중속엔진 캐파 증설을 위한 신규 투자를 공시헸다. 동사의 
기존 DC향 4행정 중속엔진 수주 단가를 고려 시 GW당 약 1조원의 매출 창출이 
가능할 전망이며 2행정 및 선박용 4행정 엔진 대비 높은 수익성이 예상되는 바 
생산이 본격화될 28년부터 동사 실적 성장성의 핵심 동인으로 작용할 전망이다.

동사에 대해 투자의견 BUY를 유지하며 업종 밸류에이션 디레이팅 및 시장 프리미엄 
조정에 따른 Target Multipe 변동으로 목표주가를 70만원으로 하향 조정한다. 상선 
시장 수주 경쟁 심화로 업종 밸류 조정이 이루어지고 있으나 동사는 엔진 기반 장기 
성장성으로 Peer 대비 상대적 매력도 확보 및 주가 방어가 가능하다는 판단이다.

Document 243: Advanced economies _ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Advanced economies _ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
History tends to repeat itself in advanced economies. Once again, growth ultimately fell short of expectations by only a small
margin in the first half of 2026, despite the conflict in Iran. As early as 2025, the impact of tariffs was less severe than feared.
This is a sign that structural factors (notably AI, defence and electrification) are underpinning growth in these countries

and are expected to continue to do so in 2027. However, there are significant risks to the economic outlook, primarily of a
geopolitical nature, with a significant upside risk to inflation and interest rates. The other instance of history repeating itself
concerns a period that those under 20 cannot have experienced: the period leading up to the 2008 crisis, echoes of which can
be seen in terms of growth, inflation, the direction of monetary policy, and long-term interest rates.

Advanced economies are experiencing trends similar to those they
faced nearly 20 years ago:
- Growth remains resilient in 2025-26 despite shocks, as it did in 2006-
07, in the major advanced economies.
- Inflation is above central banks’ targets: as in 2006-07, this calls for a
tightening of monetary policy.
- In Europe and the United States, long-term interest rates have re -
turned to their 2006-07 levels (1996 in Japan): a challenging environ-

ment for public finances in a context where public debt ratios are signi-
ficantly higher than those seen 20 years ago.
THE GLOBAL ECONOMY IN THE MIDST OF A GROWTH CYCLE
We are already well into 2026, and the picture so far is clear: glo -
bal growth is proving resilient. Following a US tariff shock last year,
growth is once again proving, for the time being, to be significantly less
affected by the conflict in Iran than had been anticipated when it first

broke out. Initially, however, there were fears that this new shock would
hit growth even harder. As with the tariff hike in 2025, the impact was
measurable, particularly in terms of consumer confidence. But in both
2025 and 2026, growth has also shown a degree of resilience.
The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise
in oil prices, the recessionary impact of which is not yet apparent.
According to our forecasts, growth was expected to reach or exceed 1%

in 2026 in the four major advanced economies (the Eurozone, Japan,
the United Kingdom and the United States, where it exceeds 2%) for
the second consecutive year. This had not happened (excluding the
post-COVID catch-up period) since 2006-07. Growth figures are more
moderate (particularly for demographic reasons), but the private sec -
tor is not (at present) showing the same excesses (at that time, the
credit boom had preceded the worst crisis since the 1930s); although

debt levels have recently risen in the US tech sector.
The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial
intelligence (AI). It is fuelling global trade and business investment
in most countries. This is clearly the case in the United States, where
business investment is growing strongly as investment plans in the
US tech sector are implemented.
However, the world’s major economies are currently engaged in a
broader push to expand their capital goods investment. Alongside the

ongoing development of services—particularly digital services—we
have seen a rebound in industrial production in most countries since
early 2025. The leading sectors are primarily technology equipment,
machinery, aeronautics, defence and their essential inputs (energy and
metals). This trend is evident everywhere, including in Europe, and
is becoming entrenched over the long term, with an improvement in
business climate indicators, particularly new export orders. Growth is

therefore expected to strengthen further in 2027, particularly in the
Eurozone, where German investment plans are set to gather momen -
tum and whose spillover effects will have a greater impact on the eco-
nomy.
However, not all sectors are experiencing the same momentum. This
is particularly true for the sectors that depend on household consump-
tion, which has shown mixed trends. Our observation that consumer
spending is more buoyant in the United States than in Europe was

confirmed in 2026, and the outlook does not point to a change in this
ranking.
EDITORIAL
RESILIENCE TO SHOCK AFTER SHOCK

### Page 2

EcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The bank
for a changing
world
While European and US households are experiencing more or less the
same energy price shock, inflation remains higher in the United States.
But this has not prevented consumer spending from keeping pace, pri-
marily because it is driven by the wealthiest households, who are less
sensitive to inflation than to the wealth effect. Furthermore, while the

savings rate remains high in the Eurozone, US households have been
drawing down their savings. This is also true of British households,
which explains why the UK has seen slightly stronger growth since
the beginning of the year. In Japan, government spending has partially
offset energy inflation (while other countries are doing so to a much
lesser extent than in 2022), which has supported consumption growth.
However, these buffers—reduced savings or fiscal support—have their

limits when shocks persist. The two major current conflicts (Iran and
Ukraine) are far from being resolved, and their impact is continuing.
This persistent geopolitical risk and its implications for inflation repre-
sent a significant headwind to the growth outlook for 2027.
HIGHER INFLATION CALLS FOR MONETARY TIGHTENING
Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks
examined in this publication, which is another similarity (albeit with

different underlying causes) with the 2006-07 period. Japan is the ex -
ception, as government support measures for households are tempo -
rarily holding back inflation.
Apart from the Bank of Japan, which is engaged in a long-term norma-
lisation process, no central bank was expected to raise its key rates
in 2026. But they are now being prompted to do so for reasons that
vary from one region to another. The first is the conflict in Iran and
the resulting rise in oil prices, which triggered a rebound in energy

inflation from the second quarter onwards. However, since the conflict
was not resolved by the memorandum of understanding signed in June,
and given that the subsequent drop in oil prices proved short-lived,
inflation is expected to rise again. The rise in gas prices is expected to
exacerbate the impact, particularly in Europe, where inventories are
being replenished ahead of winter. Electricity prices are expected to
follow suit in most countries, but at a more moderate pace than in

2022. Indeed, gas is now used less to generate electricity than it was
at that time (thanks to the expansion of renewable energy), and to an
even lesser extent in France.
There is a significant risk that energy inflation will spread further to
other components of inflation in the future, and inflation expectations
could eventually reflect this. This is why the European Central Bank
has already raised its key interest rate twice (in June and September)

and, according to our scenario, will likely do so again in December. For
the same reason, we expect the Bank of England to raise its policy rate
in November. Although the cap on energy prices was maintained for
a long time, it has now been raised. As a result, inflation is expected
to rise between now and the end of the year. The aim will then be to
prevent second-round effects.
Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength. In the

United States, it has exceeded the 2% target since 2021. The main
argument in favour of a new cycle of rate hikes (following the one in
2022-2023) lies in the current resilience of growth and the improving
labour market. The Federal Reserve (Fed) must balance two equally
important objectives: maximum employment and price stability. Howe-
ver, unlike in the 4 th quarter of 2025, when the decline in job crea -
tion justified monetary easing (by 75 basis points), the current sharp

rebound in job creation completely reverses that analysis. According
to our forecasts, the unemployment rate is expected to decrease in
the coming months, which pushes back the prospect of a decline in
core inflation. This situation persists due to both supply constraints
(over which a central bank has no control) and excess demand (which
the Fed can, however, mitigate). Consequently, the Fed raised its key
rate in September and is expected to implement two further rate hikes

between now and January 2027.
In Japan, finally, while headline inflation has been brought down by
government support measures, core inflation remains significant.
Wage pressures are mounting against a backdrop of labour shortages.
The Bank of Japan is therefore being forced to accelerate the pace of its
monetary normalisation.
In all these regions, inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026
or Q1 2027. It is against this backdrop of temporary inflation that rate

hikes could also come to an end by that time (excluding Japan). Howe-
ver, this assumes that once the energy shock—the course of which
remains uncertain—subsides, inflation will fall. The effects of El Niño,
which are still difficult to gauge at present, could take over. Inflation
would then remain above the central banks’ target for a longer period.
Under this scenario, rate hikes would go further than is currently en -
visaged.
THE RISE IN LONG- TERM INTEREST RATES: A FACTOR THAT WILL WEIGH

ON PUBLIC FINANCES
Monetary policies have also to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal
policy, particularly in the United States (where the budget deficit is
high and shows no signs of shrinking) and in Japan (where the new
government is pursuing pro-growth policies). In Europe, fiscal policy
is playing a much smaller role than it did in 2022. While the stimulus
measures implemented at that time did help reduce the inflation rate
in the short term (by nearly 2.5 pp in France, for example), by keeping

demand at a high level, they delayed disinflation and increased the
scale of the monetary tightening required.
The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: it triggered a
rise in long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the
start of the year) to levels close to those of 2006-07. This rise is wides-
pread, but its causes vary. Inflation expectations appear to play a more
significant role in the Eurozone, whereas the rise in real interest rates

seems to be the main factor in the United States and Japan.

The bank
for a changing
world
Higher inflation and more resilient real growth than feared point in
the same direction: stronger than expected nominal growth that is li -
kely to be sustained. However, in the long term, high nominal growth
tends to drive up nominal interest rates (as it is a positive sign of more
profitable investment projects), in contrast to the problem of secular

stagnation raised in the 2010s (which combined low nominal growth
with low nominal interest rates).
The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period
of growth), with various situations. In several countries, the public
debt-to-GDP ratio continues to rise. In the United States, deficit reduc-
tion does not appear to be a priority, to the extent that it is expected
to remain above 6% of GDP at the very least until the end of Donald

Trump’s term of office (with little hope that the mid-term elections will
significantly alter this trend). In the United Kingdom and France, fiscal
consolidation has begun, but it is being hampered by the simultaneous
increase in certain areas of expenditure (debt servicing, defence and
healthcare, due in particular to an ageing population), which is slowing
the pace of consolidation. In Germany, the implementation of invest -
ment plans is leading to a widening of the deficit. While this does not

pose a sustainability problem, it does result in an increase in annual
debt issuance. In Japan, the budget deficit is kept in check by interest
payments that remain low, as the rise in interest rates is recent and
the average maturity of the debt is high (10 years). However, expansio-
nary fiscal policy could pose further problems in terms of debt dyna -
mics if it were to continue through the end of the decade. In Italy and
Spain, fiscal consolidation has been sufficient to stabilise the public

debt-to-GDP ratio (and even to reduce it in Spain), but the high level of
Italy’s debt service it vulnerable to a rise in interest rates.
In all these countries, the fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it
will be in 2030. Indeed, in most cases, only half of the debt was is -
sued after 2022 (that is, after the end of the zero-interest-rate period).
The debt service has so far only partially risen, except in the United
States, where most of the increase has already occurred due to a shor-

ter average debt maturity (6 years). This means that, for all countries
combined, the rate of GDP growth remains higher than the average
interest rate paid on public debt (or the effective interest rate).
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with
GDP growth. As a result, in most cases, the debt service will have re -
turned to the level as a share of GDP seen in the early 2000s. In order to
stabilise public debt-to-GDP ratios, primary balances will need to have

improved. This will even require the generation of primary surpluses,
particularly in Italy (which is already achieving this), France and the
United Kingdom. This is a crucial shift that governments will need to
introduce as early as the 2027 budget; otherwise, they risk postponing
the stabilisation of their public debt-to-GDP ratio once again.
Article completed on 17 September 2026
Stéphane Colliac
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

Document 294: EcoNews of 19 October 2026 (2026-10-19)

Report source: BNP Paribas

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
5
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.38 / 19 October 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
IMF ANNUAL MEETINGS
Resilience, but no relief. The IMF's latest global growth forecast is un-
changed from a year ago and from April 2026 (at 3.1% in 2026 and 3.4%
in 2027), despite the energy shock, however with a different geogra -
phical breakdown: many energy importers and war-exposed countries
downgraded, and AI-exposed countries upgraded. Resilience was the

buzzword of the meetings, however it appeared mostly in the rear -
view mirror. Looking ahead, buffers are running thin, fiscal policy needs
urgent consolidation, and downside risks (from energy and AI exube -
rance) loom large. Global headline inflation is expected to rise to 4.7%
in 2026, mainly due to energy and food, before easing back to 4.1% in
2027 (revised +0.2pp). Growth in advanced economies is revised up to
2.1% in 2026 (+0.4pp, driven by Germany), staying at 1.8% in 2027. Eu-

rozone growth is upgraded to 1.1% in 2026 (+0.2pp) and 1.2% in 2027.
US growth stays projected at 2.3% in 2026 and 2027 (+0.1pp). Germany
sees the largest G7 upgrade, to 1.3% in 2026 (+0.6pp) and 1.1% in 2027
(+0.1pp). France is the only major advanced economy downgraded, to
0.4% in 2026 (-0.2pp) and 0.7% in 2027 (-0.2pp). For more detailed
takeaways see Substack and this week’s EcoWeek editorial.
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
EUROPEAN UNION
Trade policy, competitiveness and budget agreements by year-end.

EU leaders asked the Commission to propose by year-end an instru -
ment allowing, in extreme cases, full closure of the single market to
a rival deemed to be attacking the EU economy. It is designed as a
“second-strike” deterrent against unfair practices and retaliation.
The Commission is also exploring extending the hybrid-vehicle safe -
guards approach agreed with China last week to other sectors such
as chemicals and machinery. On the "Made in Europe" label, vehicles

would need to be assembled in Europe with at least 70% of compo -
nent value (excluding batteries) sourced locally and with substantial
R&D in the EU. The UK would be included, provided London aligns with
EU trade defence measures. EU Council also called for decisive pro -
gress on the competitiveness agenda, with the single-market (inclu -
ding 26th regime) and simplification files to be wrapped up by year-end.
On the multi-annual financial framework, the objective remains a deal

by end-2026, so that legislation can be adopted in 2027.
EURO AREA
Moderate tightening does it. Multiple ECB Governing Council members,
speaking from the IMF Meetings in Bangkok, re-underwrote their
“moderate response” approach to the ongoing energy-driven infla -
tion surge. Accordingly, they signaled that additional rate action was
likely but not imminent, acknowledging that the rise in bond yields
was doing part of the job, and that there were no signs yet of second

round effects. Strengthening the case for moderation, the Eurozone in-
dustrial production fell in August for the second month running, by
0.5% m/m, pulled down by pharmaceutical output and motor vehicles.
That said, production in Netherlands surged to a new high (2.5% m/m
and 8.7% y/y), driven by AI machinery and equipment.
France
Business starts booming, insolvencies are stable, growth outlook is
downgraded. Business creations rose by 16% y/y in the 3 rd quarter

(+23% y/y in September), driven mainly by the digital sector and retail.
In parallel, business bankruptcies stabilised in the 3rd quarter according
to Altares, with a 0.5% increase y/y (compared with a 5.4% increase y/y
in Q2), and their impact on employment declined: 50,510 jobs at risk
in Q3, down 3% y/y (see our recent analysis). The Banque de France
halved its estimate for Q3 GDP to 0.1% q/q (same as Insee) due to
the manufacturing sector. Its monthly survey showed a sharp jump in

business uncertainty in September, driven by fuel prices and economic
deterioration (the most acute readings in industry and services since
April). The OAT-bund spread was rangebound (137-142).
Germany
Producer prices are rising. Wholesale prices rose 8.7% y/y (vs. 6.8% in
August) due to higher wholesale prices for energy products and inter -
mediate goods, particularly metals.
UNITED KINGDOM
Summer outperformance and kudos from IMF. Monthly GDP defied

expectations in August and posted a 0.1% m/m against a -0.1% consen-
sus. This marked a slowdown from July (0.4% m/m), but also a fourth
consecutive month of expansion. Industrial production underpinned
the advance: it accelerated to 0.8% m/m (consensus: -0.1%, prior: 0.3%)
thanks to pharmaceuticals. The IMF sees growth accelerating to 1.3%
in 2026 (+0.3 pp since July) and 1.2% in 2027. It also singled out the UK
among advanced economies for its efforts and progress in putting its

fiscal house in order.
UNITED STATES
Inflation heats up, manufacturing accelerates, but an October hike
is unlikely. CPI inflation exceeded expectations at 0.65% m/m in Sep -
tember (up 0.2pp), the fastest monthly pace since April. Accordingly,
the y/y rate climbed to 3.7% (up 0.3pp). The biggest upside surprise,
however, came from the core CPI: at 0.33% m/m (consensus: 0.2%, prior:
0.3%) and 2.6% y/y (consensus: 2.5%, prior: 2.4%); as services remained

sticky while goods accelerated to 0.3% m/m on autos and AI-related
items. Producer prices too rose well above expectations to 1.3% m/m
(consensus: 0.6%, prior: 0.5%) – the largest monthly increase since
March 2022 – and 6.3% y/y (consensus: 5.3%, prior: 5.6%). Industrial
production accelerated in September to 0.7% m/m (up 0.6pp) thanks
to utilities. Manufacturing returned to growth (0.3% m/m, up 0.5pp)
with gains broadening beyond the AI-related and aerospace sectors.

Retail sales excl. autos and gasoline, by contrast, slowed sharply to
0.2% m/m (down 0.9pp). During his speech at the IMF Meetings after
the hotter than expected inflation prints, Chair Warsh declined to hint
at an imminent rate hike. We continue to expect the next rate hike to
occur in December.
To watch this week:
- Developed markets Flash PMIs for October (Friday).
- Eurozone Germany September PPI, October ZEW (Tuesday), France Insee Business Climate (Thursday).

- UK August Employment data (Tuesday), September CPI and PPI (Wednesday), September Retail Sales (Friday).
- Japan September CPI, October PMI (Friday).

JAPAN
Producer price inflation eases. Producer prices fell for a second month
in a row in September, down a further 0.5% m/m, while the yearly rate
slowed for the first time since February (-0.8pp), though it remains
firm at 6.8%. The IMF forecasts growth at 0.7% in both 2026 and 2027,
broadly unchanged. The yen weakened past 158 per dollar again after

reports that the BoJ is leaning against a back-to-back October rate hike.
EMERGING ECONOMIES
Emerging markets
Asia’s outlook upgraded, other regions downgraded.
The IMF upgraded its Asia growth forecast to 4.8% in 2026 and 4.4% in
2027. The largest upgrades are for economies well integrated into the
AI supply chain, notably Taiwan (+5.5pp to 10.7%), South Korea (+0.7pp
to 3.3%), Singapore, Vietnam, Malaysia and to some extent Thailand.
India is forecast to grow 7% in both 2026 and 2027 (fiscal year basis),

up 0.6 and 0.3pp from July on robust domestic demand.
Central Europe’s GDP growth is projected at 2.1% in 2026, slightly
lower than April’s forecast. In 2027, it remains unchanged at 2.3%.
Growth paths are diverse within the region.
In the Middle East & North Africa, GDP is expected to contract by 2%
in 2026. GCC real GDP should fall by 4.8% with only Oman expected
to post positive growth (4.1%). A marked rebound is expected for 2027
(MENA: 7%; GCC: 9.1%).

The growth outlook for Latin America remains subdued, revised down
to 2.3% in 2026 (from 2.4%) and 2.5% in 2027 (from 2.7%). However, in
its "International Trade Outlook 2026" published on 15 October, the
Economic Commission for Latin America and the Caribbean (ECLAC) ex-
pects the region to experience a trade boom in 2026: goods exports are
projected to surge by 20% in value terms (11% from price increases and
9% from volume growth), with trade to Asia (excluding China) soaring

by 32%, while intraregional trade growth lags behind at 8%. Yet, these
export gains are concentrated in commodities and low-value-added
manufacturing, limiting their spillover to broader growth. Stronger
trade is thus providing only a modest boost to the economy.
China
Explosive export growth, subdued inflation and credit growth. In Sep-
tember, export value growth surged to 36.3% y/y from 25% in August,
driven by semiconductors, computer equipment and other AI goods, as

well as green technologies. Export prices surged, especially for semi -
conductors, while total export volumes remained robust. Import value
growth also accelerated to 31% y/y from 28.2% in August, boosted by
both rising volume and rapidly increasing prices. The trade surplus
hit a new monthly record of USD 134 bn in September. CPI inflation
edged up to 0.9% y/y in September, mainly because of a 14.9% rise in
vehicle fuel prices. Food prices continued to fall (-1.7% y/y) and core

inflation was stable at 1%. Producer price inflation accelerated to 5%
(the highest since 2022) due to rising commodity prices. Growth in to-
tal domestic credit outstanding (“social financing”) continued to slow
down, reaching a record low of 7.1% y/y in September. Bank loans to
households declined (-2% y/y) for the seventh consecutive month.
In Q3 2026, real GDP growth rebounded to 4.5% y/y (1.2% q/q) from
4.3% y/y (0.9% q/q), primarily led by industrial production (which rose by

5.1% y/y on average in Q3 vs. 4.6% in Q2). On the demand side, exports
were the key driver while fixed-asset investment continued to contract.
Retail sales posted a small pickup in September but remained flat
in real terms in Q3 as a whole. With growth reaching 4.6% y/y year-to-
date, China needs only a 4.2% growth in Q4 to meet the official target of
4.5%-5% for this year – which seems easily feasible. The IMF trimmed
its growth forecast by 0.1pp to 4.5% in 2026 and 4% in 2027.

Indonesia
MSCI launched a consultation on reclassifying the country from Emer-
ging Market to Standalone Market status, with a final decision due by
end-June 2027, citing opaque shareholding structures and suspected
coordinating trading. Since January 2026, MSCI had frozen Index ad -
justments and threatened only a downgrade to Frontier market status
(like Pakistan), but market participants argued that a Frontier reclas -
sification would not properly reflect the extent of the market’s pro -

blems. Over the medium term, the risk is heavy foreign capital outflows
at a time when net FDI no longer covers the current account deficit.
This leaves Indonesia reliant on foreign portfolio inflows and the ru -
piah exposed to a reversal in sentiment. On the day of this announce -
ment, the Jakarta Composite Index fell by 11.6%, its steepest drop since
January 1998. Among Asian emerging markets, Indonesia has posted
the worst stock market performance since the beginning of the year

(-40.7%).
COMMODITIES
Iran/Oil. More of the same. Brent oil prices have remained broadly
stable this week (0.5% at 107$/b on Monday). Renewed Iranian at -
tacks on tankers in the Strait and intensification of Houthis’ attack over
the Saudi territory feed the geopolitical risk premium on barrel prices.
But IEA announcement of an accelerated 100 mb oil release (above
the March initial target of 400 mb) and reports that the US may lift
sanctions on the Russian and Iranian shadow fleets have contributed to

crude price stability. The spread with physical barrel (Dated Brent) has
declined (6.7$/b on Oct 16th). Trump administration’s various attempts
to lower diesel prices have had a limited impact for now. European gas
prices (TTF) recorded another weekly rise (4.7% w/w to 88.4 €/MWh,
the highest level since Dec 22nd, 2022) on the back of the rising number
of attacks on vessels in the Gulf and persisting disruption in the Strait.
However, EU gas inventories reached 74% of full capacities on October

16th (i.e., almost at the 80% month-end target), which should reduce
support for TTF prices going forward.
y/y = year-on-year
m/m = month-on-month
bp = basis point
pp = percentage point
mb = million barrels

tajinder.dhillon@lseg.com; 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%. 
 69.9% of companies have reported 26Q3 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q4– 27Q3) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 19.0. 
 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

There has been an increase in the share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 since the start of the quarter (to $778.4B 
from $760.7B). Three of the eleven sectors have experienced downward revisions to estimates.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-20.5%) and materials (-7%) sectors have recorded the highest percentage 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (33%) sector has recorded the highest percentage increase in earnings. 
Overall, share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 2.3% since the start of the quarter.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-14.7B) and materials (-1.2B) sectors have recorded the highest dollar-level 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (15.7B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings. 
Overall, expected share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 17.7B since the start of the quarter.

299 companies in the S&P 500 Index have reported earnings for Q3 2026. Of these companies, 81.6% reported 
earnings above analyst expectations and 15.7% reported earnings below analyst expectations. In a typical quarter 
(since 1994), 67% of companies beat estimates and 20% miss estimates. Over the past four quarters, 82% of 
companies beat the estimates and 15% missed estimates. 
In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.5% above estimates, which compares to a long-term (since 
1994) average surprise factor of 4.4% and the average surprise factor over the prior four quarters of 7.7%.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 31.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 26.4%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $778.4B in 26Q3, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $591.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q3. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q3. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Consumer 
Discretionary sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (146.2%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $63B in 26Q3, 
compared to earnings of $25.6B in 25Q3. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (566.5%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (143.8%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 39%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (68.9%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $260.2B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $154B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (137%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (71.5%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 28.2%. 
The Consumer Discretionary sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn 
$56.3B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $54.2B in 25Q3. 7 of the 18 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to 
see lower earnings than a year ago. The Casinos & Gaming (-44.1%) and Automotive Parts & Equipment (-38.8%) 
sub-industries have the lowest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate 
improves to 4.5%.

STARMINE EARNINGS SURPRISE FORECAST
Looking forward at quarterly performance, we use StarMine’s SmartEstimate® from LSEG to determine which
companies in the S&P 500 are better poised to beat and miss earnings estimates. The SmartEstimate® is a weighted

average of analyst estimates, with more weight given to more recent estimates and more accurate analysts. Our
studies have shown that when the SmartEstimate® differs significantly from the consensus (I/B/E/S mean estimate),
the Predicted Surprise accurately predicts the direction of earnings surprises or further revisions 70% of the time.
When a significant Predicted Surprise for revenue is also present for the period, the accuracy improves to 78%.

StarMine ARM from LSEG is an analyst revisions stock ranking model, designed to predict future changes in analyst
sentiment. ARM incorporates more accurate earnings estimates through the SmartEstimate prediction service. ARM
region rankings scores companies by region on a scale of 1 to 100 where 100 represents the most bullish sentiment.
Over the next two weeks, 147 S&P 500 companies are expected to report earnings. Of these companies, 17 positive

surprises and 11 negative surprises are expected from S&P 500 companies reporting quarterly results.

### Page 1

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: Who can be benefited if neocloud has no cash to invest?
Date:    37 minutes ago
Who can be benefited if neocloud has no cash to invest?

Who can be beneﬁted if
neocloud has no cash to
invest?
當投資⼈擔⼼新興雲因淨負債惡化沒錢加碼資本開⽀跟上軍備競賽⽽
下調半導體產業鏈預期，但持有龐⼤ AI 數據算⼒中⼼的舊雲應該反⽽
會受惠
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
NOV 1 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
Executive summary
之前曾多次提到如果 AI 數據算⼒中⼼資本開⽀減速（請參考  May 17, 2026

深度報告：Likely an inﬂection point if AI data center capital density
to reverse down?）， AI 半導體產業鏈將受到影響⽽  underperform ，所以我
們覺得美國費城半導體指數在 10 ⽉份近 15% 的回檔，應該也正是在反應市場
已經開始擔⼼ AI 數據算⼒中⼼，尤其是  Neocloud 新興外溢雲，像是
CoreWeave ，未來將因缺乏⾜夠現⾦流，可能將無法跟上其他算⼒中⼼公司
的 AI 數據算⼒中⼼資本開⽀的軍備競賽，但在此同時，⼤語⾔模型，真實世界
模型，及 AI 代理⼈算⼒需求持續增加，每 GW 算⼒租⽤合約價格調漲有利於能
持續加碼投資的 AI 數據算⼒中⼼（請參考：17-21% On-demand AI GPU
pricing hike to shift value from LLM companies to cloud vendors）。
所以我們的看法是 AI 數據算⼒中⼼資本開⽀因資⾦不⾜被下修， capital

density to reverse down ，這些雖然對半導體產業鏈不利，但反⽽對倖存的
AI 數據算⼒中⼼有利， CoreWeave 沒錢加碼投資，也不代表短期會⾯臨倒閉
⾵險，反⽽表⽰其現有的算⼒及基礎建設更值錢。

### Page 2

3Q26 total sales and cloud sales comparison
歸因於成⻑引擎雲業務佔⽐仍低， 5 ⼤⽼牌 AI 數據算⼒中⼼ 3Q26 整體營收季
度僅增⻑ 1-6% （ vs. SpaceXSI’s 87% qoq) ，年增僅 12-30% （ vs.
SpaceXSI’s 161% yoy) 。這其中還是以⾕歌的 25% ， Meta 的 26% ，甲⾻⽂
的 30% 年增表現較為突出。但光就 AI 數據中⼼雲業務營收來分析，還是⾕歌的
24% 季增及 103% 年增及 SpaceXSI 的 195% 季增及 830% 年增明顯優於同
業，⾕歌以⾃研 TPU 為主來建⽴其訓練及推理 AI 數據中⼼， SpaceXSI 是以
Nvidia GPU 為主來建⽴其訓練及推理 AI 數據中⼼。

微軟⼩幅優於同業。歸因於⾕歌雲整體營收佔⽐仍低於 30% ，外加⾮核⼼事業
虧損⼀堆，⾕歌短期整體營業利潤率反⽽逐季惡化，但公司未來透過博通增加
TPU 及機櫃銷售，外加增加存儲記憶體從三星， SK Hynix, 美光⾃⾏採購及直
接將⾃研運算晶⽚  Compute die 下單給台積電來減少博通税，我們認為⾕歌

雲業務  2027-2028 營業利潤率有機會超過 40% ，增加雲業務⽐重也會拉升整
體營業利潤率。臉書 2Q26-3Q26 因⼀次性罰款影響營業利潤率，這也會在
4Q26 有明顯改善。

Capital return on cloud sales and proﬁts
improvement
除了  Oracle 的雲業務的  capital return 在 3Q26 沒有明顯改善外，⾕歌雲業
務的資本開⽀營收回報率從上季度的 55% ，回升到 67% ，亞⾺遜的 AWS ，微
軟的 Azure 也有 4 個百分點的改善，⽽ SpaceXSI 有近 100 個百分點的改善。
從各家雲業務的資本開⽀獲利回報率來看，明顯改善的有 SpaceXSI 的 98 個百
分點，微軟  Azure 的 10 個百分點，⼩幅改善的有⾕歌的 2 個百分點，甲⾻⽂
無改善，亞⾺遜的 AWS 竟然退步的 5 個百分點，我們歸因於 AWS 雲業務的季
度及年度營收增⻑明顯低於同業。
Capital density (capex/sales) might peak in 2H26-
1H27

即使各⼤ AI 數據中⼼算⼒中⼼於 2027 年將持續加碼資本開⽀超過 30% 年增，
讓其  capital density 續創新⾼，但我們重申這次  AI 數據算⼒中⼼基礎建設投
資⼤週期的  capital density 應該會在  2H26-1H27 ⾒到⾼點。以 3Q26 來
看，⾕歌， Meta, 微軟三家公司其 capital density 都季增 2-3 個百分點，亞
⾺遜的 capital density 季增近 10 個百分點，甲⾻⽂跟 SpaceXSI 都是⼤幅增
加， 4Q26 應該會持續創新⾼。

利差優勢消長，新興與商品
貨幣走勢各異
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
高油價、美元偏強及全球景氣分化持續牽動新興與
商品貨幣。澳洲就業轉弱、通膨改善，澳洲央行升
息週期料暫告一段落，澳幣短線料區間盤整。紐西
蘭央行意外按兵不動削弱紐幣短期動能，但升息預
期仍提供支撐。加拿大央行政策轉鷹及高油價利好
加幣，惟經濟與貿易不確定性限制升值空間。中國
內需偏弱，但出口暢旺、企業結匯需求強，加上政
策對升值容忍度提高，人民幣料溫和升值。南非幣
受美元、高油價及利差收窄壓抑，但財政紀律改善、
信用前景轉佳及實質利率優勢有助緩和貶值壓力，
料維持區間震盪。
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望
澳幣 AUDUSD 0.71 景氣放緩、澳央轉向觀望，澳幣短線偏向盤整

紐幣 NZDUSD 0.59 美元強勢壓抑紐元表現，惟中期復甦動能仍在
加幣 USDCAD 1.39 央行轉鷹支撐加幣，惟經濟及貿易風險限制升值空間
人民幣 USDCNY 6.65 人民幣迎結匯需求與中美利差擴大的拉鋸
南非幣 USDZAR 16.20 南非幣短線承壓，利差優勢支撐中期前景

《投資關鍵 – 澳洲景氣放緩、澳央轉向觀望，預期
澳幣盤整》
隨著國際油價維持高位，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至 4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。
但隨著高利率與高通膨壓力持續拖累經濟動能，近期
公布的 9月就業意外減少 3.93萬人 、失業率更升至
4.7%，已連續兩個月落在澳央主席認為可有效壓抑
通膨的失業率4.5~5.0%區間；通膨方面，9月份澳洲
CPI雖由4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以
住房通膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本
輪升息暫時告終 ，2027年政策轉向觀望，如國際油
價回落至90美元以下，澳央有降息空間。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不

振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有
限，加以預期聯準會在 12月再度升息 ，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整，要進一步突破或要待2Q27美國
通膨回落、降息預期再度升溫，方有走揚力量。
重點摘要
• 經濟基本面降溫 ，核心CPI穩
定，澳洲央行暫停升息轉向
觀望 ，澳幣維持在 0.7~0.71
中性盤整。

紐西蘭通膨升溫訊號浮現，月度物價年增 5.2%
《投資關鍵 - 央行意外按兵不動拖累紐元 ，後續升息
預期提供下檔支撐》
過去一個月 ，受美元強勢 、美債殖利率及油價走高拖
累，紐幣兌美元一度下探 0.5565，其後隨升息預期升
溫而回穩 。惟紐西蘭央行 10月28日意外維持 OCR於
2.75%不變，促使市場下修短期升息預期 ，紐幣再度
承壓。
紐西蘭經濟延續溫和復甦 ，企業信心及農產品價格改
善，惟勞動市場仍有閒置產能 。雖然整體通膨低於預
期，但核心通膨及價格廣度上升 ，加上油價高企與紐

幣偏弱，我們預期4Q26通膨仍具黏性，價格壓力擴散
風險猶存。
我們仍維持偏鷹派的政策基準情境 。10月以4比3票按
兵不動，較反映升息時點後移 ，而非整體政策路徑轉
向，緊縮週期尚未結束。我們仍預期12月及明年3月具
升息空間 ，尤其若通膨 、油價及匯率壓力持續 ，政策
立場料維持偏緊。
展望4Q26，10月意外按兵不動削弱紐幣利差支撐，我
們仍看好紐元中期修復空間 ，惟短期上行動能有所減
弱。升息預期降溫 ，加上大選 、美元及全球風險情緒
等不確定性，料使匯價維持較高波動。進入1Q27後，
隨政策不確定性逐步下降 ，經濟溫和復甦有望成為紐
幣主要支撐 。考量匯價已反映相當程度的成長疑慮及
外部利空 ，若基本面持續改善 ，紐幣中期仍具修復空
間。
• 展望4Q26，10月意外按兵不動
削弱紐幣利差支撐 ，我們仍看
好紐元中期修復空間 ，惟短期
上行動能有所減弱。
• 隨政策不確定性逐步下降 ，經
濟溫和復甦有望成為紐幣主要
支撐 。考量匯價已反映相當程
度的成長疑慮及外部利空 ，若
基本面持續改善 ，紐幣中期仍

具修復空間。
資料來源：StatsNZ， GS，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/21
紐西蘭通膨
按月CPI（約50%項目）
按月核心CPI（約45%項目）
按季CPI（全部項目）

### Page 4

通膨數據回落，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要 《投資關鍵 - 央行轉鷹支撐加幣，惟經濟及貿易風險
限制升值空間》
能源價格高企令加拿大通膨面臨上行風險，加拿大央
行政策預期轉鷹亦推動加美利差劣勢收窄，支持加幣
自10月中旬低點反彈 。不過，隨本次25個基點升息
已大致反映於市場定價，加幣能否延續升勢將更取決
於央行對後續緊縮路徑的指引，以及市場對終端利率
的重新定價，而非本次升息本身。
加拿大央行最新升息 25個基點至2.5%，為逾三年來
首次升息，並表示若能源價格進一步推升廣泛通膨壓
力，不排除繼續收緊政策 。央行亦將 2027年通膨預
測由 2.0%上調至 3.1%，並將今年經濟成長預測由
0.7%上調至1.0%，短期政策訊號偏鷹 。惟核心通膨
仍相對溫和，勞動市場、製造業需求及招聘意願亦呈
轉弱跡象，意味若經濟活動進一步降溫，持續升息空
間或有限。高油價及偏高的投機性加幣空頭部位仍構
成短期利好，但美加貿易不確定性可能繼續拖累企業
投資及成長前景。因此，我們維持4Q26至1Q27加幣

中性看法。短期加美利差收窄及油價高企有望限制加
幣下行風險，但在增長前景改善或央行釋出更明確的
持續緊縮訊號前，加幣進一步升值空間有限。

資料來源：Bloomberg，Commerzbank Research，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理, 2026/10
• 我們對 4Q26至1Q27 加幣整體
維持中性看法 ，短期利差收窄
料為加幣提供一定支撐 ，惟在
經濟動能及貿易前景轉佳前 ，
升值空間仍然有限。

### Page 5

，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要
• 從景氣趨勢、政策取向與國
際經貿局勢變化三大視角切
入，綜合判斷人民幣溫和升
值的看法不變。
《投資關鍵 – 政經拉鋸與景氣K型下，人民幣溫和升值》
中國公布最新 3Q26 GDP成長4.5%，出口年增率升至
28.4%，貿易順差由前季的3148.6億美元擴大至3651.2
億美元 。市場預估中國 2026年全年貿易順差將超越
2025年1.2兆美元紀錄，再創新高。惟中國外需繁榮卻
無法掩蓋內需疲軟困境 ，中國3Q26零售銷售年增率僅
0.7%，固定資產投資衰退7%。儘管中國陸續推出穩增
長政策，大多以財政政策為主軸，貨幣政策偏向定向式
而非全面降準降息。因此儘管美啟動升息，中美利差尚
保持穩定，因此內需疲軟與有限度的寬鬆貨幣政策對人
民幣走勢並未形成顯著壓力。
中國的出口暢旺除受惠科技週期貢獻外，強勁汽車出口
至歐洲也是主因，9月中國汽車出口維持62%的高成長。
惟加劇保護主義傾向，中歐達成初步協議，未來四年內
將對歐洲的混合動力車出口量減少一半以上，具體配額、

最低售價及豁免條件仍待協商。政策角度觀察，人民幣
升值本身可能成為中國應對外部壓力的重要工具，減緩
低估匯率促進出口的國際批評。
考量關稅可能成為攻防戰手段之一，研判中國廠商或搶
出口支持外貿表現高檔，同時官方可能對人民幣升值空
間容忍態度提高 ，疊加前三季結售匯順差高達 3,941億
美元，創2010年以來新高，顯示企業結匯意願強勁，持
續為人民幣提供支撐。從景氣趨勢、政策取向與國際經
貿局勢變化的三大視角切入，綜合判斷人民幣溫和升值
的看法不變。

### Page 6

《投資關鍵 - 基本面改善但部位趨擁擠，南非幣料維
持震盪》
南非幣第四季以來震盪偏弱，USD/ZAR近期回升至約
16.60。外部環境仍偏不利，高油價推升進口成本與
通膨壓力，加上美元偏強及美國南非利差收窄，削弱
南非幣套息吸引力。與此同時，巴西總統大選結果引
發市場波動，增添新興市場不確定性，短期投資人風
險偏好轉趨審慎，亦可能加重新興市場貨幣的資金流
出壓力。
基本面方面，南非財政紀律改善、主權信用前景轉佳，
加上較高實質利率及具吸引力的利差，仍為南非幣提
供支撐。惟高油價及聖嬰現象相關極端天氣風險令通
膨壓力偏高，限制政策寬鬆空間。另一方面，近期投
機性及資產管理機構多頭部位明顯增加，顯示市場部
位漸趨擁擠。在外部風險升溫及風險偏好轉弱下，獲
利了結與部位調整可能放大短期波動，即使匯價回檔，
逢低布局的風險報酬吸引力仍相對有限。
整體而言，南非幣基本面有所改善，但短期外部環境
與部位面均偏不利。財政紀律改善、主權信用前景轉
佳及實質利率優勢有助建立匯率下檔支撐，惟強勢美
元、高油價、利差收窄及偏擁擠的多頭部位限制升值

空間。預期4Q26美元兌南非幣仍以區間震盪為主，維
持南非幣中性看法。
重點摘要
資料來源：CFTC，Bloomberg，2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。

 東海経済は、持ち直している
 7月の生産（東海3県、季節調整値）は前月
比＋2.2％と2ヵ月ぶりに増加した。生産水
準も高く、増加基調にあるとみられる
 輸出は総じてみると足踏み状態。先行きは
持ち直しを見込むが、米・イランの対立長
期化などで足踏みとなるリスクがある
 日銀短観9月調査の業況判断DI（「良い」

増加が見込まれている

5ヵ月連続で前年比プラスとなった

年を上回った。
